// FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/ui/viewmodel/AttendanceViewModel.kt
======================================================================
package com.example.ui.viewmodel

import android.content.Intent
import android.util.Log
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.example.data.local.OfflineAttendanceStore
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceLog
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceTimeRules
import com.example.data.model.Employee
import com.example.data.model.FaceAnalysisFrame
import com.example.data.model.FaceBiometricMatcher
import com.example.data.model.LivenessDirection
import com.example.data.model.LivenessEvaluator
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import com.example.data.report.AttendanceEmailReporter
import com.example.data.report.EmailReportStatus
import com.example.data.repository.AttendanceRepository
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.catch
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.collectLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import java.util.Calendar

sealed interface TerminalUiPhase {
    data object Ready : TerminalUiPhase

    data class Scanning(
        val attempt: Int,
        val statusHeadline: String,
        val cameraReady: Boolean = false,
        val faceDetected: Boolean = false,
        val eyesLocked: Boolean = false,
        val noseMustacheLocked: Boolean = false,
        val jawBeardLocked: Boolean = false,
        val livenessSteps: List<LivenessDirection> = listOf(
            LivenessDirection.TURN_LEFT,
            LivenessDirection.TURN_RIGHT,
            LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY
        ),
        val currentLivenessStepIndex: Int = 0,
        val qualityHint: String? = null
    ) : TerminalUiPhase

    data class Verified(
        val employee: Employee,
        val eventType: AttendanceEventType,
        val verificationMethod: VerificationMethod,
        val confidenceScore: Int,
        val timestampLabel: String,
        val syncedToCloud: Boolean,
        val logId: String = "",
        val liveSelfiePath: String? = null,
        val deviceSenderAccount: String? = null,
        val emailReportStatus: EmailReportStatus = EmailReportStatus.NEEDS_CONFIG
    ) : TerminalUiPhase

    data class Failure(
        val title: String,
        val message: String
    ) : TerminalUiPhase
}

sealed interface DataUiState<out T> {
    data object Loading : DataUiState<Nothing>
    data class Success<T>(val data: T) : DataUiState<T>
    data class Error(val message: String) : DataUiState<Nothing>
}

data class ClockSnapshot(
    val timeLabel: String = AttendanceTimeRules.formatTimeLabel(),
    val dateKey: String = AttendanceTimeRules.formatDateKey(),
    val eventType: AttendanceEventType = AttendanceTimeRules.currentEventType(),
    val windowLabel: String = AttendanceTimeRules.windowDescription(
        Calendar.getInstance().get(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY)
    )
)

class AttendanceViewModel(
    private val offlineStore: OfflineAttendanceStore,
    private val repository: AttendanceRepository? = null,
    private val currentUserId: String? = null,
    private val emailReporter: AttendanceEmailReporter? = null,
    autoInitBackgroundJobs: Boolean = true,
    private val calendarProvider: () -> Calendar = { Calendar.getInstance() },
    private val continuousKioskMode: Boolean = true,
    private val sameEmployeeCooldownMs: Long = 3_000L
) : ViewModel() {

    val employeesState: StateFlow<DataUiState<List<Employee>>> =
        offlineStore.employees
            .map<List<Employee>, DataUiState<List<Employee>>> { DataUiState.Success(it) }
            .stateIn(
                scope = viewModelScope,
                started = SharingStarted.Eagerly,
                initialValue = DataUiState.Success(offlineStore.employees.value)
            )

    val logsState: StateFlow<DataUiState<List<AttendanceLog>>> =
        offlineStore.logs
            .map<List<AttendanceLog>, DataUiState<List<AttendanceLog>>> { DataUiState.Success(it) }
            .stateIn(
                scope = viewModelScope,
                started = SharingStarted.Eagerly,
                initialValue = DataUiState.Success(offlineStore.logs.value)
            )

    val pendingSyncIds: StateFlow<Set<String>> = offlineStore.pendingSyncLogIds

    private val _cloudSyncError = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val cloudSyncError: StateFlow<String?> = _cloudSyncError.asStateFlow()

    private val _terminalPhase = MutableStateFlow<TerminalUiPhase>(
        if (autoInitBackgroundJobs) {
            TerminalUiPhase.Scanning(
                attempt = 1,
                statusHeadline = "TERMINAL SCANNER ACTIVE",
                cameraReady = false,
                livenessSteps = listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY),
                currentLivenessStepIndex = 0
            )
        } else {
            TerminalUiPhase.Ready
        }
    )
    val terminalPhase: StateFlow<TerminalUiPhase> = _terminalPhase.asStateFlow()

    private val _clock = MutableStateFlow(buildClockSnapshot(calendarProvider()))
    val clock: StateFlow<ClockSnapshot> = _clock.asStateFlow()

    private fun buildClockSnapshot(now: Calendar): ClockSnapshot {
        val hour = now.get(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY)
        return ClockSnapshot(
            timeLabel = AttendanceTimeRules.formatTimeLabel(now),
            dateKey = AttendanceTimeRules.formatDateKey(now),
            eventType = AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(hour),
            windowLabel = AttendanceTimeRules.windowDescription(hour)
        )
    }

    private var scanTimerJob: Job? = null
    private var resetTimerJob: Job? = null
    private var emailDispatchJob: Job? = null
    private var scanMatchInFlight = false
    private var consecutiveMatchCount = 0
    private var livenessHoldCount = 0
    private var lastMatchedEmployeeId: String? = null
    private var lastVerifiedEmployeeId: String? = null
    private var lastVerifiedTimestampMs: Long = 0L
    private var latestCapturedSelfiePath: String? = null
    private var analyzedFaceFrames = 0
    private var rejectedFaceFrames = 0
    private var highestRejectedConfidence = 0

    init {
        if (autoInitBackgroundJobs) {
            viewModelScope.launch {
                offlineStore.calibrateRegisteredReferenceFaces()
            }

            if (repository != null && !currentUserId.isNullOrBlank()) {
                startAuthenticatedCloudSync(repository, currentUserId)
            } else {
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    retryPendingEmailReports()
                }
            }

            viewModelScope.launch {
                while (isActive) {
                    _clock.value = buildClockSnapshot(calendarProvider())
                    delay(1000L)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun startAuthenticatedCloudSync(repo: AttendanceRepository, uid: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.ensureStarterDirectoryIfEmpty(offlineStore.employees.value)
                .onFailure { err ->
                    _cloudSyncError.value = err.localizedMessage
                }
            syncPendingChangesInternal(repo)
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.observeEmployees(uid)
                .catch { error ->
                    val firestoreError = generateSequence(error) { it.cause }
                        .filterIsInstance<FirebaseFirestoreException>()
                        .firstOrNull()
                    if (firestoreError == null && error is CancellationException) throw error
                    val root = firestoreError ?: error
                    Log.w(TAG, "Cloud employee listener error", root)
                    _cloudSyncError.value = root.localizedMessage
                }
                .collectLatest { remoteEmployees ->
                    _cloudSyncError.value = null
                    offlineStore.mergeRemoteEmployees(remoteEmployees)
                }
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.observeAttendanceLogs(uid)
                .catch { error ->
                    val firestoreError = generateSequence(error) { it.cause }
                        .filterIsInstance<FirebaseFirestoreException>()
                        .firstOrNull()
                    if (firestoreError == null && error is CancellationException) throw error
                    val root = firestoreError ?: error
                    Log.w(TAG, "Cloud log listener error", root)
                    _cloudSyncError.value = root.localizedMessage
                }
                .collectLatest { remoteLogs ->
                    _cloudSyncError.value = null
                    offlineStore.mergeRemoteLogs(remoteLogs)
                }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun syncPendingChangesInternal(repo: AttendanceRepository) {
        val pendingDeletes = offlineStore.pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value.toList()
        for (empId in pendingDeletes) {
            repo.deleteEmployee(empId)
                .onSuccess { offlineStore.markEmployeeDeleteSynced(empId) }
                .onFailure { err -> _cloudSyncError.value = err.localizedMessage }
        }

        val employeesById = offlineStore.employees.value.associateBy { it.id }
        val pendingEmployees = offlineStore.pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value.toList()
        for (empId in pendingEmployees) {
            val emp = employeesById[empId]
            if (emp == null) {
                offlineStore.markEmployeeSynced(empId)
                continue
            }
            repo.upsertEmployee(emp, overwriteIfExists = true)
                .onSuccess { offlineStore.markEmployeeSynced(empId) }
                .onFailure { err -> _cloudSyncError.value = err.localizedMessage }
        }

        val pendingIds = offlineStore.pendingSyncLogIds.value
        if (pendingIds.isEmpty()) {
            retryPendingEmailReports()
            return
        }

        val allEmployees = offlineStore.employees.value.associateBy { it.id }
        val pendingLogs = offlineStore.logs.value.filter { it.id in pendingIds }.reversed()
        for (log in pendingLogs) {
            val emp = allEmployees[log.employeeId] ?: Employee(
                id = log.employeeId,
                employeeCode = log.employeeCode,
                name = log.employeeName,
                department = log.department,
                roleTitle = "Authorized Staff",
                passcode = "147",
                avatarKey = log.avatarKey,
                isActive = true
            )
            repo.upsertEmployee(emp, overwriteIfExists = false)
            val res = repo.logAttendance(
                employee = emp,
                eventType = AttendanceEventType.fromWire(log.eventType),
                verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.fromWire(log.verificationMethod),
                confidenceScore = log.confidenceScore.toInt(),
                loggedTimeLabel = log.loggedTimeLabel,
                loggedDateKey = log.loggedDateKey,
                explicitLogId = log.id
            )
            if (res.isSuccess) {
                offlineStore.markLogSynced(log.id)
            } else {
                _cloudSyncError.value = res.exceptionOrNull()?.localizedMessage
            }
        }
        retryPendingEmailReports()
    }

    private suspend fun retryPendingEmailReports() {
        val reporter = emailReporter ?: return
        val pendingEmails = offlineStore.pendingEmailLogIds.value.toList()
        if (pendingEmails.isEmpty()) return

        val employeesById = offlineStore.employees.value.associateBy { it.id }
        val logsById = offlineStore.logs.value.associateBy { it.id }
        val selfieMap = offlineStore.getSelfiePathsByLogId()
        val recentLogs = offlineStore.logs.value
        val pendingCloudIds = offlineStore.pendingSyncLogIds.value

        for (logId in pendingEmails) {
            val log = logsById[logId]
            if (log == null) {
                offlineStore.markEmailSent(logId)
                continue
            }
            val employee = employeesById[log.employeeId] ?: Employee(
                id = log.employeeId,
                employeeCode = log.employeeCode,
                name = log.employeeName,
                department = log.department,
                roleTitle = "Authorized Staff",
                avatarKey = log.avatarKey
            )
            val outcome = reporter.generateAndSendReport(
                employee = employee,
                log = log,
                eventType = AttendanceEventType.fromWire(log.eventType),
                verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.fromWire(log.verificationMethod),
                confidenceScore = log.confidenceScore.toInt(),
                syncedToCloud = log.id !in pendingCloudIds,
                recentDailyLogs = recentLogs,
                liveSelfiePath = selfieMap[log.id]
            )
            if (outcome == EmailReportStatus.SENT || outcome == EmailReportStatus.NEEDS_CONFIG) {
                offlineStore.markEmailSent(logId)
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Launches continuous hands-free biometric face scanning with automatic background
     * directional & liveness verification and 1% match sensitivity.
     */
    fun startVipBiometricScan() {
        startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
    }

    fun startVipBiometricScan(
        livenessSequence: List<LivenessDirection>,
        preserveCameraReady: Boolean = false
    ) {
        val normalizedSequence = if (livenessSequence.isEmpty()) {
            listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY)
        } else {
            livenessSequence
        }
        scanTimerJob?.cancel()
        resetTimerJob?.cancel()
        scanMatchInFlight = false
        consecutiveMatchCount = 0
        livenessHoldCount = 0
        lastMatchedEmployeeId = null
        latestCapturedSelfiePath = null
        analyzedFaceFrames = 0
        rejectedFaceFrames = 0
        highestRejectedConfidence = 0

        _terminalPhase.value = TerminalUiPhase.Scanning(
            attempt = 1,
            statusHeadline = "TERMINAL SCANNER ACTIVE",
            cameraReady = preserveCameraReady,
            livenessSteps = normalizedSequence,
            currentLivenessStepIndex = 0
        )
    }

    /**
     * Begins analysis timing only after CameraX hardware binding succeeds cleanly.
     * In continuous kiosk mode, stays ready inside Scanning standby indefinitely without blocking UI.
     */
    fun onCameraInitialized() {
        val current = _terminalPhase.value as? TerminalUiPhase.Scanning ?: return
        if (current.cameraReady) return

        val firstInstruction = current.livenessSteps
            .getOrElse(current.currentLivenessStepIndex) { LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY }
            .instruction

        _terminalPhase.update { phase ->
            if (phase is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning) {
                phase.copy(
                    cameraReady = true,
                    statusHeadline = "LIVENESS 1/${phase.livenessSteps.size} • $firstInstruction"
                )
            } else {
                phase
            }
        }

        if (continuousKioskMode) return

        scanTimerJob?.cancel()
        scanTimerJob = viewModelScope.launch {
            delay(ATTEMPT_DURATION_MS)
            if (_terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning && !scanMatchInFlight) {
                _terminalPhase.update { phase ->
                    if (phase is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning) {
                        val activeStep = phase.livenessSteps
                            .getOrElse(phase.currentLivenessStepIndex) { LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY }
                        phase.copy(
                            attempt = 2,
                            statusHeadline = "AUTO-RETRY (2/2) • ${activeStep.instruction}"
                        )
                    } else {
                        phase
                    }
                }
                delay(ATTEMPT_DURATION_MS)
                if (_terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning && !scanMatchInFlight) {
                    val activePhase = _terminalPhase.value as? TerminalUiPhase.Scanning
                    val livenessIncomplete = activePhase != null &&
                        activePhase.currentLivenessStepIndex < activePhase.livenessSteps.lastIndex
                    val failureMessage = when {
                        livenessIncomplete ->
                            "Directional liveness sequence was not completed in time. Please follow the on-screen arrows and try again."
                        rejectedFaceFrames > 0 ->
                            "Facial geometry did not match any enrolled staff member (${highestRejectedConfidence}% match). Ensure good lighting and retry."
                        analyzedFaceFrames > 0 ->
                            "Could not lock eyes, nose, and jawline cleanly. Look straight into the camera and retry."
                        else ->
                            "No live face detected inside the scanner reticle. Position your face clearly and retry."
                    }
                    _terminalPhase.value = TerminalUiPhase.Failure(
                        title = "Face Verification Incomplete",
                        message = failureMessage
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun onCameraInitFailed(reason: String) {
        val current = _terminalPhase.value as? TerminalUiPhase.Scanning ?: return
        if (current.cameraReady || scanMatchInFlight) return
        scanTimerJob?.cancel()
        _terminalPhase.value = TerminalUiPhase.Failure(
            title = "Camera Hardware Unavailable",
            message = "Could not initialize camera sensor ($reason). Check permissions and retry."
        )
    }

    fun onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(frame: FaceAnalysisFrame) {
        val current = _terminalPhase.value as? TerminalUiPhase.Scanning ?: return

        _terminalPhase.update { phase ->
            if (phase is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning) {
                phase.copy(
                    faceDetected = frame.faceDetected,
                    eyesLocked = frame.eyesLocked,
                    noseMustacheLocked = frame.noseMustacheLocked,
                    jawBeardLocked = frame.jawBeardLocked,
                    qualityHint = frame.qualityIssue
                )
            } else {
                phase
            }
        }

        if (!frame.selfieImagePath.isNullOrBlank()) {
            latestCapturedSelfiePath = frame.selfieImagePath
        }

        if (!frame.faceDetected || scanMatchInFlight) return

        val steps = current.livenessSteps
        val stepIndex = current.currentLivenessStepIndex.coerceIn(0, (steps.size - 1).coerceAtLeast(0))
        val activeDirection = steps.getOrElse(stepIndex) { LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY }

        // Evaluate directional liveness step before advancing to frontal biometric match & selfie capture
        if (activeDirection != LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY) {
            val directionPassed = LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = activeDirection,
                headYawDeg = frame.headYawDeg,
                headPitchDeg = frame.headPitchDeg,
                isFrontCamera = frame.isFrontCamera
            )
            if (directionPassed) {
                livenessHoldCount++
                if (livenessHoldCount >= LIVENESS_HOLD_FRAMES) {
                    livenessHoldCount = 0
                    val nextIndex = (stepIndex + 1).coerceAtMost(steps.lastIndex)
                    val nextDirection = steps[nextIndex]
                    _terminalPhase.update { phase ->
                        if (phase is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning) {
                            phase.copy(
                                currentLivenessStepIndex = nextIndex,
                                statusHeadline = "LIVENESS ${nextIndex + 1}/${steps.size} • ${nextDirection.instruction}",
                                qualityHint = null
                            )
                        } else {
                            phase
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                livenessHoldCount = 0
            }
            return
        }

        // Verify background directional pose alignment + eye liveness before matching
        val frontalDirectionSatisfied = LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
            direction = LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY,
            headYawDeg = frame.headYawDeg,
            headPitchDeg = frame.headPitchDeg,
            isFrontCamera = frame.isFrontCamera
        )
        if (!frontalDirectionSatisfied) {
            consecutiveMatchCount = 0
            return
        }

        val liveSignature = frame.signature
        if (liveSignature.isNullOrBlank()) {
            consecutiveMatchCount = 0
            return
        }

        analyzedFaceFrames++
        val employees = offlineStore.employees.value
        val evaluation = FaceBiometricMatcher.evaluateFrame(liveSignature, employees)
        val match = evaluation.match

        if (match == null) {
            consecutiveMatchCount = 0
            lastMatchedEmployeeId = null
            rejectedFaceFrames++
            if (evaluation.bestConfidencePercent > highestRejectedConfidence) {
                highestRejectedConfidence = evaluation.bestConfidencePercent
            }
            return
        }

        val nowMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
        if (sameEmployeeCooldownMs > 0L &&
            match.employee.id == lastVerifiedEmployeeId &&
            (nowMs - lastVerifiedTimestampMs) < sameEmployeeCooldownMs
        ) {
            return
        }

        if (lastMatchedEmployeeId == match.employee.id) {
            consecutiveMatchCount++
        } else {
            lastMatchedEmployeeId = match.employee.id
            consecutiveMatchCount = 1
        }

        // Instant match at >= 1% confidence without secondary passcode or waiting loops
        if (consecutiveMatchCount >= REQUIRED_CONFIRMING_FRAMES ||
            match.confidencePercent >= FaceBiometricMatcher.MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT
        ) {
            scanMatchInFlight = true
            scanTimerJob?.cancel()
            val method = if (current.attempt == 1) {
                VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1
            } else {
                VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_2
            }
            val capturedSelfie = frame.selfieImagePath ?: latestCapturedSelfiePath

            viewModelScope.launch {
                commitVerifiedAttendance(
                    employee = match.employee,
                    method = method,
                    confidence = match.confidencePercent,
                    explicitEventType = null,
                    liveSelfiePath = capturedSelfie
                )
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun commitVerifiedAttendance(
        employee: Employee,
        method: VerificationMethod,
        confidence: Int,
        explicitEventType: AttendanceEventType?,
        liveSelfiePath: String? = null
    ) {
        val now = calendarProvider()
        val eventType = explicitEventType ?: AttendanceTimeRules.currentEventType(now)
        val timeLabel = AttendanceTimeRules.formatTimeLabel(now)
        val dateKey = AttendanceTimeRules.formatDateKey(now)
        lastVerifiedEmployeeId = employee.id
        lastVerifiedTimestampMs = System.currentTimeMillis()

        // Persist immediately to local storage so check-in/out never freezes the UI
        val localEntry = offlineStore.recordLocalAttendance(
            employee = employee,
            eventType = eventType,
            verificationMethod = method,
            confidenceScore = confidence,
            loggedTimeLabel = timeLabel,
            loggedDateKey = dateKey,
            synced = false
        )
        val durableSelfiePath = offlineStore.persistSelfieForLog(localEntry.id, liveSelfiePath)
            ?: liveSelfiePath

        val senderAccount = emailReporter?.resolveSenderAccountEmail()

        _terminalPhase.value = TerminalUiPhase.Verified(
            employee = employee,
            eventType = eventType,
            verificationMethod = method,
            confidenceScore = confidence,
            timestampLabel = timeLabel,
            syncedToCloud = false,
            logId = localEntry.id,
            liveSelfiePath = durableSelfiePath,
            deviceSenderAccount = senderAccount,
            emailReportStatus = EmailReportStatus.NEEDS_CONFIG
        )

        // Sync to Firebase Firestore in background without blocking the verified confirmation
        val repo = repository
        if (repo != null && !currentUserId.isNullOrBlank()) {
            viewModelScope.launch {
                runCatching {
                    repo.upsertEmployee(employee)
                    repo.logAttendance(
                        employee = employee,
                        eventType = eventType,
                        verificationMethod = method,
                        confidenceScore = confidence,
                        loggedTimeLabel = timeLabel,
                        loggedDateKey = dateKey,
                        explicitLogId = localEntry.id
                    )
                }.onSuccess { cloudResult ->
                    if (cloudResult.isSuccess) {
                        offlineStore.markLogSynced(localEntry.id)
                        _terminalPhase.update { phase ->
                            if (phase is TerminalUiPhase.Verified && phase.logId == localEntry.id) {
                                phase.copy(syncedToCloud = true)
                            } else {
                                phase
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        _cloudSyncError.value = cloudResult.exceptionOrNull()?.localizedMessage
                    }
                }.onFailure { err ->
                    _cloudSyncError.value = err.localizedMessage
                }
            }
        }

        // Check-In: keep simple (no email button, auto-return to scanning).
        // Check-Out: keep Verified overlay visible so employee can tap Send Email & Finish.
        if (eventType == AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN) {
            scheduleAutoResetToReady()
        }
    }

    /**
     * Dispatches the individual attendance PDF report and live selfie photo
     * to Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com when the employee taps Send Email & Finish
     * after a recorded Check-Out, then returns the terminal to active Scanning.
     */
    fun sendEmailAndFinish(onLaunchEmailIntent: (Intent) -> Unit) {
        val current = _terminalPhase.value as? TerminalUiPhase.Verified ?: run {
            resetToReady()
            return
        }
        if (current.emailReportStatus == EmailReportStatus.DISPATCHING) return

        resetTimerJob?.cancel()

        val reporter = emailReporter
        if (reporter == null) {
            resetToReady()
            return
        }

        val matchingLog = buildLogForVerifiedPhase(current)
        val snapshotLogs = offlineStore.logs.value

        if (current.emailReportStatus == EmailReportStatus.SENT) {
            resetToReady()
            return
        }

        _terminalPhase.update { phase ->
            if (phase is TerminalUiPhase.Verified && phase.logId == current.logId) {
                phase.copy(emailReportStatus = EmailReportStatus.DISPATCHING)
            } else {
                phase
            }
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            var dispatchedViaSmtp = false
            if (reporter.hasConfiguredSmtpCredentials()) {
                val outcome = reporter.generateAndSendReport(
                    employee = current.employee,
                    log = matchingLog,
                    eventType = current.eventType,
                    verificationMethod = current.verificationMethod,
                    confidenceScore = current.confidenceScore,
                    syncedToCloud = current.syncedToCloud,
                    recentDailyLogs = snapshotLogs,
                    liveSelfiePath = current.liveSelfiePath
                )
                if (outcome == EmailReportStatus.SENT) {
                    offlineStore.markEmailSent(matchingLog.id)
                    retryPendingEmailReports()
                    dispatchedViaSmtp = true
                }
            }

            if (!dispatchedViaSmtp) {
                runCatching {
                    val pdfFile = reporter.generateReportPdfFile(
                        employee = current.employee,
                        log = matchingLog,
                        eventType = current.eventType,
                        verificationMethod = current.verificationMethod,
                        confidenceScore = current.confidenceScore,
                        syncedToCloud = current.syncedToCloud,
                        recentDailyLogs = snapshotLogs,
                        liveSelfiePath = current.liveSelfiePath
                    )
                    val gmailIntent = reporter.buildDeviceGmailShareIntent(
                        pdfFile = pdfFile,
                        employee = current.employee,
                        log = matchingLog,
                        eventType = current.eventType,
                        verificationMethod = current.verificationMethod,
                        confidenceScore = current.confidenceScore,
                        syncedToCloud = current.syncedToCloud
                    )
                    offlineStore.markEmailSent(matchingLog.id)
                    onLaunchEmailIntent(gmailIntent)
                }.onFailure { err ->
                    Log.w(TAG, "Failed to launch Gmail share intent: ${err.message}")
                }
            }
            resetToReady()
        }
    }

    fun sendVerifiedReportToEmail() {
        val current = _terminalPhase.value as? TerminalUiPhase.Verified ?: return
        if (emailDispatchJob?.isActive == true &&
            current.emailReportStatus == EmailReportStatus.DISPATCHING
        ) {
            return
        }

        resetTimerJob?.cancel()

        val reporter = emailReporter
        if (reporter == null) {
            _terminalPhase.update { phase ->
                if (phase is TerminalUiPhase.Verified && phase.logId == current.logId) {
                    phase.copy(emailReportStatus = EmailReportStatus.NEEDS_CONFIG)
                } else {
                    phase
                }
            }
            scheduleAutoResetToReady()
            return
        }

        _terminalPhase.update { phase ->
            if (phase is TerminalUiPhase.Verified && phase.logId == current.logId) {
                phase.copy(emailReportStatus = EmailReportStatus.DISPATCHING)
            } else {
                phase
            }
        }

        val matchingLog = buildLogForVerifiedPhase(current)
        launchEmailReportDispatch(
            employee = current.employee,
            log = matchingLog,
            eventType = current.eventType,
            verificationMethod = current.verificationMethod,
            confidence = current.confidenceScore,
            syncedToCloud = current.syncedToCloud,
            liveSelfiePath = current.liveSelfiePath,
            rescheduleResetAfter = true
        )
    }

    private fun buildLogForVerifiedPhase(current: TerminalUiPhase.Verified): AttendanceLog {
        return offlineStore.logs.value.firstOrNull { it.id == current.logId }
            ?: AttendanceLog(
                id = current.logId.ifBlank { "manual_${System.currentTimeMillis()}" },
                employeeId = current.employee.id,
                employeeCode = current.employee.employeeCode,
                employeeName = current.employee.name,
                department = current.employee.department,
                avatarKey = current.employee.avatarKey,
                eventType = current.eventType.wireValue,
                verificationMethod = current.verificationMethod.wireValue,
                confidenceScore = current.confidenceScore.toLong().coerceIn(0L, 100L),
                loggedTimeLabel = current.timestampLabel,
                loggedDateKey = AttendanceTimeRules.formatDateKey()
            )
    }

    private fun launchEmailReportDispatch(
        employee: Employee,
        log: AttendanceLog,
        eventType: AttendanceEventType,
        verificationMethod: VerificationMethod,
        confidence: Int,
        syncedToCloud: Boolean,
        liveSelfiePath: String? = null,
        rescheduleResetAfter: Boolean = false
    ) {
        val reporter = emailReporter ?: return
        val snapshotLogs = offlineStore.logs.value
        emailDispatchJob?.cancel()
        emailDispatchJob = viewModelScope.launch {
            val outcome = reporter.generateAndSendReport(
                employee = employee,
                log = log,
                eventType = eventType,
                verificationMethod = verificationMethod,
                confidenceScore = confidence,
                syncedToCloud = syncedToCloud,
                recentDailyLogs = snapshotLogs,
                liveSelfiePath = liveSelfiePath
            )
            if (outcome == EmailReportStatus.QUEUED_OFFLINE) {
                offlineStore.markEmailPending(log.id)
            } else if (outcome == EmailReportStatus.SENT) {
                offlineStore.markEmailSent(log.id)
                retryPendingEmailReports()
            }
            _terminalPhase.update { current ->
                if (current is TerminalUiPhase.Verified && current.logId == log.id) {
                    current.copy(emailReportStatus = outcome)
                } else {
                    current
                }
            }
            if (rescheduleResetAfter) {
                scheduleAutoResetToReady()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun scheduleAutoResetToReady() {
        resetTimerJob?.cancel()
        resetTimerJob = viewModelScope.launch {
            delay(VERIFIED_AUTO_RESET_MS)
            if (continuousKioskMode) {
                startVipBiometricScan(
                    livenessSequence = listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY),
                    preserveCameraReady = true
                )
            } else {
                resetToReady()
            }
        }
    }

    fun resetToReady() {
        scanTimerJob?.cancel()
        resetTimerJob?.cancel()
        scanMatchInFlight = false
        consecutiveMatchCount = 0
        livenessHoldCount = 0
        lastMatchedEmployeeId = null
        latestCapturedSelfiePath = null
        _terminalPhase.value = if (continuousKioskMode) {
            TerminalUiPhase.Scanning(
                attempt = 1,
                statusHeadline = "TERMINAL SCANNER ACTIVE",
                cameraReady = true,
                livenessSteps = listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY),
                currentLivenessStepIndex = 0
            )
        } else {
            TerminalUiPhase.Ready
        }
    }

    override fun onCleared() {
        super.onCleared()
        scanTimerJob?.cancel()
        resetTimerJob?.cancel()
    }

    private companion object {
        const val TAG = "AttendanceVM"
        const val ATTEMPT_DURATION_MS = 7000L
        const val VERIFIED_AUTO_RESET_MS = 2500L
        const val REQUIRED_CONFIRMING_FRAMES = 1
        const val LIVENESS_HOLD_FRAMES = 1
    }
}

// FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/base/FirestoreEmulatorTestBase.kt
======================================================================
package com.example.base

import android.content.Context
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.example.R
import com.google.firebase.FirebaseApp
import com.google.firebase.FirebaseOptions
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuthInvalidUserException
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreSettings
import com.google.firebase.firestore.MemoryCacheSettings
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeout
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
import org.robolectric.annotation.LooperMode

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(manifest = Config.NONE)
@LooperMode(LooperMode.Mode.INSTRUMENTATION_TEST)
abstract class FirestoreEmulatorTestBase {

    protected lateinit var firestore: FirebaseFirestore
    protected lateinit var auth: FirebaseAuth
    protected lateinit var databaseId: String

    @Before
    open fun setUpFirebase() {
        val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Context>()
        val resId = context.resources.getIdentifier("firestore_database_id", "string", context.packageName)
        databaseId = if (resId != 0) {
            context.getString(resId)
        } else {
            "ai-studio-android-nichatte-7f6d200a-1161-49bd-b79a-93905545b7f7"
        }

        val app = if (FirebaseApp.getApps(context).isEmpty()) {
            val newApp = FirebaseApp.initializeApp(
                context,
                FirebaseOptions.Builder()
                    .setApplicationId("com.aistudio.nichattendance.vtxkqm")
                    .setProjectId(PROJECT_ID)
                    .setApiKey("fake-api-key-for-emulator")
                    .build()
            )
            val newFirestore = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(newApp, databaseId)
            newFirestore.useEmulator(EMULATOR_HOST, FIRESTORE_PORT)
            newFirestore.firestoreSettings = FirebaseFirestoreSettings.Builder()
                .setLocalCacheSettings(MemoryCacheSettings.newBuilder().build())
                .build()

            val newAuth = FirebaseAuth.getInstance(newApp)
            newAuth.useEmulator(EMULATOR_HOST, AUTH_PORT)
            newApp
        } else {
            FirebaseApp.getInstance()
        }

        firestore = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(app, databaseId)
        auth = FirebaseAuth.getInstance(app)
    }

    @After
    open fun tearDownFirebase() {
        auth.signOut()
    }

    protected suspend fun signInTestUser(email: String): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        withTimeout(AUTH_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            val result = try {
                auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD).await()
            } catch (unused: FirebaseAuthInvalidUserException) {
                auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD).await()
            }
            checkNotNull(result.user?.uid) { "User auth failed" }
        }
    }

    protected fun Throwable.unwrapFirestoreException(): FirebaseFirestoreException? {
        var current: Throwable? = this
        while (current != null) {
            if (current is FirebaseFirestoreException) return current
            current = current.cause
        }
        return null
    }

    private companion object {
        const val EMULATOR_HOST = "127.0.0.1"
        const val FIRESTORE_PORT = 8085
        const val AUTH_PORT = 9099
        const val PROJECT_ID = "demo-no-project"
        const val DEFAULT_PASSWORD = "password123"
        const val AUTH_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000L
    }
}

// FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/data/local/OfflineAttendanceStoreTest.kt
======================================================================
package com.example.data.local

import android.content.Context
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.Employee
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(sdk = [36])
class OfflineAttendanceStoreTest {

    private lateinit var context: Context

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext()
        context.getSharedPreferences("nich_offline_attendance", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit()
            .clear()
            .commit()
    }

    @Test
    fun initialSeed_includesMuhammadAnsarWithDualCodes() {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val ansar = store.employees.value.firstOrNull { it.id == OfflineAttendanceStore.MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID }

        assertNotNull(ansar)
        assertEquals("147", ansar?.checkInCode)
        assertEquals("369", ansar?.checkOutCode)
        assertEquals("avatar_ansar", ansar?.avatarKey)

        val checkInMatch = store.verifyCode("147", AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, checkInMatch?.eventType)
        assertEquals(OfflineAttendanceStore.MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID, checkInMatch?.employee?.id)

        val checkOutMatch = store.verifyCode("369", AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, checkOutMatch?.eventType)
        assertEquals(OfflineAttendanceStore.MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID, checkOutMatch?.employee?.id)
    }

    @Test
    fun offlineAttendanceAndEmployeeEdits_surviveRemoteMergeUntilSynced() {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val ansar = store.employees.value.first { it.id == OfflineAttendanceStore.MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID }

        val localLog = store.recordLocalAttendance(
            employee = ansar,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.PASSCODE,
            confidenceScore = 100,
            loggedTimeLabel = "09:15:00 AM",
            loggedDateKey = "2026-11-13",
            synced = false
        )
        assertTrue(localLog.id in store.pendingSyncLogIds.value)

        val updatedSig = "0.4100:0.4600:0.6200:0.7400"
        store.updateEmployeeFaceSignature(ansar, updatedSig)
        assertTrue(ansar.id in store.pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value)

        // Simulate stale cloud snapshot arriving before pending queue flushes
        val staleRemoteEmployee = ansar.copy(faceSignature = "0.1111:0.2222:0.3333:0.4444")
        store.mergeRemoteEmployees(listOf(staleRemoteEmployee))
        val afterMerge = store.employees.value.first { it.id == ansar.id }
        assertEquals(updatedSig, afterMerge.faceSignature)

        // Simulate empty/older cloud logs arriving while offline log is pending
        store.mergeRemoteLogs(emptyList())
        assertTrue(store.logs.value.any { it.id == localLog.id })

        // Verify persistence across store recreation
        val reloaded = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        assertTrue(localLog.id in reloaded.pendingSyncLogIds.value)
        assertTrue(ansar.id in reloaded.pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value)

        reloaded.markLogSynced(localLog.id)
        reloaded.markEmployeeSynced(ansar.id)
        assertFalse(localLog.id in reloaded.pendingSyncLogIds.value)
        assertFalse(ansar.id in reloaded.pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value)
    }

    @Test
    fun offlineDelete_preventsRemoteResurrectionUntilDeleteSynced() {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val target = Employee(
            id = "emp_temp_delete",
            employeeCode = "NICH-8888",
            name = "Temp Staff",
            department = "Security",
            roleTitle = "Officer",
            passcode = "8888",
            isActive = true
        )
        store.mergeRemoteEmployees(listOf(target))
        assertTrue(store.employees.value.any { it.id == target.id })

        store.deleteEmployee(target.id, queueCloudDelete = true)
        assertTrue(target.id in store.pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value)
        assertFalse(store.employees.value.any { it.id == target.id })

        store.mergeRemoteEmployees(listOf(target))
        assertFalse(store.employees.value.any { it.id == target.id })

        store.markEmployeeDeleteSynced(target.id)
        assertFalse(target.id in store.pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value)
    }
}

package com.example.data.model

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test

class AttendanceLogicTest {

    @Test
    fun determineEventType_morningHours_returnsCheckIn() {
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(8))
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(9))
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(11))
    }

    @Test
    fun determineEventType_noonAndAfternoonHours_returnsCheckOut() {
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(12))
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(14))
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(18))
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(23))
    }

    @Test
    fun faceBiometricMatcher_matchesCloseAndPartialVectorsAtOnePercentThreshold() {
        val enrolled = listOf(
            Employee(
                id = "emp_1",
                name = "Dr. Ananya Rao",
                faceSignature = "0.3600:0.4200:0.6500:0.7800",
                isActive = true
            )
        )

        val closeMatch = FaceBiometricMatcher.matchBestEmployee("0.3650:0.4220:0.6480:0.7820", enrolled)
        assertNotNull(closeMatch)
        assertEquals("emp_1", closeMatch?.employee?.id)

        val partialMatch = FaceBiometricMatcher.matchBestEmployee("0.5900:0.7500:0.3500:0.9500", enrolled)
        assertNotNull(partialMatch)
        assertEquals("emp_1", partialMatch?.employee?.id)
        assertEquals(true, (partialMatch?.confidencePercent ?: 0) >= 1)
        assertEquals(true, (closeMatch?.confidencePercent ?: 0) > (partialMatch?.confidencePercent ?: 0))

        assertNull(FaceBiometricMatcher.matchBestEmployee("0.3650:0.4220:0.6480:0.7820", emptyList()))
    }

    @Test
    fun faceBiometricMatcher_analyzesFullEightFeatureBeardMustacheEyeVectorsWithOnePercentFloor() {
        val enrolledFull = listOf(
            Employee(
                id = "emp_muhammad_ansar",
                name = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
                faceSignature = FaceBiometricMatcher.encodeFullSignature(
                    eyeToBoxRatio = 0.435f,
                    noseToEyeRatio = 0.480f,
                    mouthToEyeRatio = 0.640f,
                    boxAspectRatio = 0.750f,
                    philtrumToEyeRatio = 0.280f,
                    jawChinDropRatio = 0.760f,
                    jawToCheekWidthRatio = 0.730f,
                    symmetryBalanceRatio = 0.950f
                ),
                isActive = true
            )
        )

        val alignedFull = FaceBiometricMatcher.encodeFullSignature(
            eyeToBoxRatio = 0.440f,
            noseToEyeRatio = 0.485f,
            mouthToEyeRatio = 0.635f,
            boxAspectRatio = 0.755f,
            philtrumToEyeRatio = 0.285f,
            jawChinDropRatio = 0.750f,
            jawToCheekWidthRatio = 0.740f,
            symmetryBalanceRatio = 0.945f
        )
        val matched = FaceBiometricMatcher.matchBestEmployee(alignedFull, enrolledFull)
        assertNotNull(matched)
        assertEquals("emp_muhammad_ansar", matched?.employee?.id)

        val partialOrDistantStructure = FaceBiometricMatcher.encodeFullSignature(
            eyeToBoxRatio = 0.310f,
            noseToEyeRatio = 0.620f,
            mouthToEyeRatio = 0.450f,
            boxAspectRatio = 0.910f,
            philtrumToEyeRatio = 0.410f,
            jawChinDropRatio = 0.510f,
            jawToCheekWidthRatio = 0.920f,
            symmetryBalanceRatio = 0.760f
        )
        val flexibleMatch = FaceBiometricMatcher.matchBestEmployee(partialOrDistantStructure, enrolledFull)
        assertNotNull(flexibleMatch)
        assertEquals("emp_muhammad_ansar", flexibleMatch?.employee?.id)
        assertEquals(true, (flexibleMatch?.confidencePercent ?: 0) >= 1)
    }

    @Test
    fun passcodeResolver_resolvesDedicatedCheckInAndCheckOutCodes() {
        val employees = listOf(
            Employee(
                id = "emp_muhammad_ansar",
                employeeCode = "NICH-0147",
                name = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
                passcode = "147",
                checkInCode = "147",
                checkOutCode = "369",
                isActive = true
            ),
            Employee(
                id = "emp_nich_1042",
                employeeCode = "NICH-1042",
                name = "Dr. Ananya Rao",
                passcode = "1042",
                isActive = true
            )
        )

        val checkIn = PasscodeResolver.resolve("147", employees, AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT)
        assertNotNull(checkIn)
        assertEquals("emp_muhammad_ansar", checkIn?.employee?.id)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, checkIn?.eventType)

        val checkOut = PasscodeResolver.resolve("369", employees, AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN)
        assertNotNull(checkOut)
        assertEquals("emp_muhammad_ansar", checkOut?.employee?.id)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, checkOut?.eventType)

        val fallbackTime = PasscodeResolver.resolve("1042", employees, AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT)
        assertNotNull(fallbackTime)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, fallbackTime?.eventType)

        assertNull(PasscodeResolver.resolve("9999", employees, AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN))
    }

    @Test
    fun livenessEvaluator_validatesDirectionalHeadPosesAndSequence() {
        val sequence = LivenessDirection.buildChallengeSequence(seed = 42L)
        assertEquals(3, sequence.size)
        assertEquals(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY, sequence.last())
        assertEquals(true, sequence[0] != sequence[1])

        assertEquals(
            true,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.TURN_LEFT,
                headYawDeg = 14f,
                headPitchDeg = 0f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
        assertEquals(
            false,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.TURN_LEFT,
                headYawDeg = -14f,
                headPitchDeg = 0f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
        assertEquals(
            true,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.TURN_RIGHT,
                headYawDeg = -13f,
                headPitchDeg = 0f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
        assertEquals(
            true,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.LOOK_UP,
                headYawDeg = 0f,
                headPitchDeg = 12f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
        assertEquals(
            true,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.LOOK_DOWN,
                headYawDeg = 0f,
                headPitchDeg = -11f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
        assertEquals(
            true,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY,
                headYawDeg = 3f,
                headPitchDeg = -2f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun bidiTextHelper_isolatesMixedEnglishUrduWithoutReversingParentheses() {
        val formatted = BidiTextHelper.formatEmployeeHeaderTitle(
            rawName = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
            employeeCode = "NICH-0147"
        )
        assertEquals(true, formatted.contains("\u2067محمد انصار\u2069"))
        assertEquals("Muhammad Ansar / محمد انصار - NICH-0147", BidiTextHelper.stripBidiControls(formatted))
    }
}

// FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/data/report/AttendanceReportTest.kt

package com.example.data.report

import android.content.Context
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceLog
import com.example.data.model.Employee
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.InputStreamReader
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.ServerSocket
import java.net.Socket
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(sdk = [36])
class AttendanceReportTest {

    @Test
    fun generateReportPdf_createsValidPdfFile() {
        val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Context>()
        val employee = Employee(
            id = "emp_muhammad_ansar",
            employeeCode = "NICH-0147",
            name = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
            department = "Executive Operations",
            roleTitle = "Authorized Staff",
            passcode = "147",
            checkInCode = "147",
            checkOutCode = "369"
        )
        val log = AttendanceLog(
            id = "log_pdf_test_001",
            employeeId = employee.id,
            employeeCode = employee.employeeCode,
            employeeName = employee.name,
            department = employee.department,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN.wireValue,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.PASSCODE.wireValue,
            confidenceScore = 100L,
            loggedTimeLabel = "09:10:00 AM",
            loggedDateKey = "2026-11-15"
        )

        val selfieFile = java.io.File(context.cacheDir, "unit_test_selfie.jpg")
        val bmp = android.graphics.Bitmap.createBitmap(64, 64, android.graphics.Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        java.io.FileOutputStream(selfieFile).use { stream ->
            bmp.compress(android.graphics.Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 85, stream)
        }
        bmp.recycle()

        val pdfFile = AttendancePdfGenerator.generateReportPdf(
            context = context,
            employee = employee,
            log = log,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.PASSCODE,
            confidenceScore = 100,
            syncedToCloud = false,
            recentDailyLogs = listOf(log),
            recipientEmail = AttendanceEmailReporter.TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL,
            liveSelfiePath = selfieFile.absolutePath
        )

        try {
            assertTrue(pdfFile.exists())
            assertTrue(pdfFile.length() > 200L)
            val rawBytes = pdfFile.readBytes()
            val header = rawBytes.copyOfRange(0, 5).toString(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
            assertEquals("%PDF-", header)
            val pdfContentText = rawBytes.toString(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
            assertTrue(pdfContentText.contains("unit_test_selfie.jpg") || rawBytes.size > 1500)
        } finally {
            pdfFile.delete()
            selfieFile.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun smtpEmailSender_executesCompleteMimeAttachmentHandshake() {
        val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Context>()
        val employee = Employee(
            id = "emp_ansar",
            employeeCode = "NICH-0147",
            name = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
            department = "Executive Operations",
            roleTitle = "Authorized Staff"
        )
        val log = AttendanceLog(
            id = "log_smtp_test_001",
            employeeId = employee.id,
            employeeCode = employee.employeeCode,
            employeeName = employee.name,
            department = employee.department,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT.wireValue,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1.wireValue,
            confidenceScore = 97L,
            loggedTimeLabel = "05:30:00 PM",
            loggedDateKey = "2026-11-15"
        )
        val pdfFile = AttendancePdfGenerator.generateReportPdf(
            context = context,
            employee = employee,
            log = log,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1,
            confidenceScore = 97,
            syncedToCloud = true,
            recentDailyLogs = listOf(log),
            recipientEmail = AttendanceEmailReporter.TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL
        )

        val selfieFile = java.io.File(context.cacheDir, "smtp_unit_selfie.jpg")
        val bmp = android.graphics.Bitmap.createBitmap(48, 48, android.graphics.Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        java.io.FileOutputStream(selfieFile).use { stream ->
            bmp.compress(android.graphics.Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 85, stream)
        }
        bmp.recycle()

        val serverSocket = ServerSocket(0, 1, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"))
        val receivedCommands = mutableListOf<String>()
        val dataPayload = StringBuilder()
        val serverDone = CountDownLatch(1)

        val serverThread = Thread {
            serverSocket.use { srv ->
                srv.accept().use { client ->
                    val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(client.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
                    val writer = OutputStreamWriter(client.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)

                    fun reply(msg: String) {
                        writer.write("$msg\r\n")
                        writer.flush()
                    }

                    reply("220 localhost ESMTP ready")
                    var inData = false
                    while (true) {
                        val line = reader.readLine() ?: break
                        if (inData) {
                            if (line == ".") {
                                inData = false
                                reply("250 2.0.0 OK queued")
                            } else {
                                dataPayload.append(line).append('\n')
                            }
                        } else {
                            receivedCommands += line
                            when {
                                line.startsWith("EHLO") -> reply("250-localhost\r\n250 AUTH LOGIN")
                                line == "AUTH LOGIN" -> reply("334 VXNlcm5hbWU6")
                                receivedCommands.count { it == "AUTH LOGIN" } == 1 &&
                                    receivedCommands.size >= 3 &&
                                    receivedCommands[receivedCommands.size - 2] == "AUTH LOGIN" -> reply("334 UGFzc3dvcmQ6")
                                line.startsWith("MAIL FROM:") -> reply("250 2.1.0 OK")
                                line.startsWith("RCPT TO:") -> reply("250 2.1.5 OK")
                                line == "DATA" -> {
                                    reply("354 End data with <CR><LF>.<CR><LF>")
                                    inData = true
                                }
                                line == "QUIT" -> {
                                    reply("221 Bye")
                                    break
                                }
                                else -> reply("235 2.7.0 Authentication successful")
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            serverDone.countDown()
        }
        serverThread.start()

        try {
            val sender = SmtpEmailSender { cfg ->
                Socket(cfg.host, cfg.port)
            }
            sender.sendWithAttachment(
                config = SmtpConfig(
                    host = "127.0.0.1",
                    port = serverSocket.localPort,
                    useSsl = false,
                    username = "Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com",
                    appPassword = "abcd efgh ijkl mnop"
                ),
                toEmail = AttendanceEmailReporter.TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL,
                subject = "NICH Attendance 3 • CHECK-OUT • محمد انصار",
                plainTextBody = "Attendance verified for NICH-0147.",
                attachmentFile = pdfFile,
                extraAttachmentFile = selfieFile
            )

            assertTrue(serverDone.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
            assertTrue(receivedCommands.any { it == "MAIL FROM:<Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com>" })
            assertTrue(receivedCommands.any { it == "RCPT TO:<Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com>" })
            val rawMime = dataPayload.toString()
            assertTrue(rawMime.contains("Content-Type: application/pdf; name=\"${pdfFile.name}\""))
            assertTrue(rawMime.contains("Content-Type: image/jpeg; name=\"${selfieFile.name}\""))
            assertTrue(rawMime.contains("Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64"))
        } finally {
            pdfFile.delete()
            selfieFile.delete()
            runCatching { serverSocket.close() }
        }
    }
}

// FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/data/repository/AttendanceRepositoryRuleTest.kt

package com.example.data.repository

import com.example.base.FirestoreEmulatorTestBase
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceTimeRules
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeout
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Assert.fail
import org.junit.Test
import java.util.UUID

class AttendanceRepositoryRuleTest : FirestoreEmulatorTestBase() {

    @Test
    fun createEmployeeAndVerifyPasscode_authenticatedOwner_succeeds() = runBlocking {
        val uid = signInTestUser(ALICE_EMAIL)
        val repository = AttendanceRepository(firestore, auth)
        val uniquePasscode = (100..999).random().toString()
        val uniqueOutCode = (100..999).random().toString()
        val uniqueCode = "NICH-${UUID.randomUUID().toString().take(6)}"

        val createResult = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.createEmployee(
                employeeCode = uniqueCode,
                name = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
                department = "Executive Operations",
                roleTitle = "Authorized Staff",
                passcode = uniquePasscode,
                avatarKey = "avatar_ansar",
                faceSignature = "0.4350:0.4800:0.6400:0.7500",
                checkInCode = uniquePasscode,
                checkOutCode = uniqueOutCode
            )
        }
        assertTrue(createResult.isSuccess)
        val employeeId = createResult.getOrThrow()
        assertTrue(employeeId.isNotBlank())

        val verifiedIn = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.verifyEmployeeByPasscode(uniquePasscode)
        }
        assertTrue(verifiedIn.isSuccess)
        assertEquals("محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)", verifiedIn.getOrThrow()?.name)

        val verifiedOut = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.verifyEmployeeByPasscode(uniqueOutCode)
        }
        assertTrue(verifiedOut.isSuccess)
        assertEquals(employeeId, verifiedOut.getOrThrow()?.id)

        val emitted = withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.observeEmployees(uid).first { list -> list.any { it.id == employeeId } }
        }
        assertTrue(emitted.any { it.id == employeeId })
    }

    @Test
    fun logAttendance_validEmployee_createsImmutableLog() = runBlocking {
        val uid = signInTestUser(ALICE_EMAIL)
        val repository = AttendanceRepository(firestore, auth)

        val employeeId = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.createEmployee(
                employeeCode = "NICH-${UUID.randomUUID().toString().take(6)}",
                name = "Marcus Chen",
                department = "Neural Systems",
                roleTitle = "Principal Architect",
                passcode = (1000..9999).random().toString(),
                avatarKey = "avatar_2"
            ).getOrThrow()
        }
        val employee = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.getEmployeeById(employeeId).getOrThrow()!!
        }

        val logResult = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.logAttendance(
                employee = employee,
                eventType = AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(9),
                verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_2,
                confidenceScore = 95,
                loggedTimeLabel = "09:30:00 AM",
                loggedDateKey = "2026-11-13"
            )
        }
        assertTrue(logResult.isSuccess)
        val logId = logResult.getOrThrow()

        val logs = withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.observeAttendanceLogs(uid).first { list -> list.any { it.id == logId } }
        }
        val createdLog = logs.first { it.id == logId }
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN.wireValue, createdLog.eventType)
        assertEquals(VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_2.wireValue, createdLog.verificationMethod)
    }

    @Test
    fun getEmployeeById_crossUserAccess_failsWithPermissionDenied() = runBlocking {
        val aliceUid = signInTestUser(ALICE_EMAIL)
        val aliceRepo = AttendanceRepository(firestore, auth)
        val employeeId = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            aliceRepo.createEmployee(
                employeeCode = "NICH-CROSS",
                name = "Elena Vance",
                department = "Operations",
                roleTitle = "Director",
                passcode = "3095",
                avatarKey = "avatar_3"
            ).getOrThrow()
        }

        val bobUid = signInTestUser(BOB_EMAIL)
        check(bobUid != aliceUid)

        try {
            withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
                firestore.collection("users").document(aliceUid)
                    .collection("employees").document(employeeId)
                    .get()
                    .await()
            }
            fail("Expected PERMISSION_DENIED for cross-user read")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            val fsEx = e.unwrapFirestoreException()
            assertNotNull("Expected FirebaseFirestoreException, got $e", fsEx)
            assertEquals(FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.PERMISSION_DENIED, fsEx?.code)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun observeEmployees_unauthenticatedUser_failsWithPermissionDenied() = runBlocking {
        val aliceUid = signInTestUser(ALICE_EMAIL)
        auth.signOut()
        val repository = AttendanceRepository(firestore, auth)

        try {
            withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
                repository.observeEmployees(aliceUid).first()
            }
            fail("Expected PERMISSION_DENIED for unauthenticated observeEmployees")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            val fsEx = e.unwrapFirestoreException()
            assertNotNull("Expected nested FirebaseFirestoreException, got $e", fsEx)
            assertEquals(FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.PERMISSION_DENIED, fsEx?.code)
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val ALICE_EMAIL = "alice.nich@test.com"
        const val BOB_EMAIL = "bob.nich@test.com"
        const val DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000L
        const val FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000L
    }
}

package com.example.ui.viewmodel

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertIsDisplayed

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
