class AttendanceViewModelStrictTest {

    @get:Rule
    val composeRule = createComposeRule()

    private val testDispatcher = StandardTestDispatcher()
    private lateinit var context: Context

    private fun morningCheckInCalendar(): Calendar = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
        clear()
        set(2026, Calendar.NOVEMBER, 14, 9, 15, 0)
    }

    private fun afternoonCheckOutCalendar(): Calendar = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
        clear()
        set(2026, Calendar.NOVEMBER, 14, 17, 30, 0)
    }

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        Dispatchers.setMain(testDispatcher)
        context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext()
        context.getSharedPreferences("nich_offline_attendance", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit()
            .clear()
            .commit()
    }

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        Dispatchers.resetMain()
    }

    @Test
    fun terminalStartsScanningImmediatelyAndRemainsLiveInContinuousKioskMode() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val vmStore = androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelStore()
        val viewModel = androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider(
            vmStore,
            object : androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider.Factory {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                override fun <T : androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel> create(modelClass: Class<T>): T {
                    return AttendanceViewModel(
                        offlineStore = store,
                        autoInitBackgroundJobs = true,
                        continuousKioskMode = true
                    ) as T
                }
            }
        )[AttendanceViewModel::class.java]

        try {
            runCurrent()

            val initialPhase = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
            assertTrue(initialPhase is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
            assertEquals(false, (initialPhase as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning).cameraReady)

            // Advance clock 10 seconds while camera is initializing: must NOT time out early
            advanceTimeBy(10_000L)
            runCurrent()
            assertTrue(viewModel.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)

            // CameraX binds -> remains live in continuous standby without manual retry buttons
            viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()
            assertTrue((viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning).cameraReady)

            viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(FaceAnalysisFrame(faceDetected = false))
            advanceTimeBy(20_000L)
            runCurrent()
            val standby = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
            assertTrue(standby is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
            assertTrue((standby as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning).cameraReady)
        } finally {
            vmStore.clear()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun checkIn_doesNotSendEmailAndAutoResetsToScanningAfterConfirmation() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val dispatchCount = AtomicInteger(0)
        val smtpSender = SmtpEmailSender { _ ->
            dispatchCount.incrementAndGet()
            throw IllegalStateException("Check-In must never dispatch email")
        }
        val reporter = AttendanceEmailReporter(
            context = context,
            smtpSender = smtpSender,
            senderEmailProvider = { "Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com" },
            appPasswordProvider = { "abcd efgh ijkl mnop" }
        )
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
            offlineStore = store,
            emailReporter = reporter,
            autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
            calendarProvider = { morningCheckInCalendar() },
            continuousKioskMode = true,
            sameEmployeeCooldownMs = 0L
        )
        runCurrent()

        viewModel.startVipBiometricScan()
        viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
        runCurrent()

        viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(
            FaceAnalysisFrame(
                faceDetected = true,
                eyesLocked = true,
                signature = OfflineAttendanceStore.DEFAULT_ANSAR_FACE_SIGNATURE,
                selfieImagePath = "/tmp/live_selfie_auto_return.jpg"
            )
        )
        runCurrent()

        val verified = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
        assertTrue(verified is TerminalUiPhase.Verified)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, (verified as TerminalUiPhase.Verified).eventType)
        assertEquals(0, dispatchCount.get())

        // After short ~2.5s Verification Successful overlay, automatically returns to terminal-ready Scanning mode
        advanceTimeBy(2_600L)
        runCurrent()
        val returned = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
        assertTrue(returned is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
        assertTrue((returned as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning).cameraReady)
        assertEquals(0, dispatchCount.get())
    }

    @Test
    fun vipScan_acceptsMinimalOnePercentPartialFaceSimilarityImmediatelyWithSelfie() = runTest {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
            offlineStore = store,
            autoInitBackgroundJobs = false
        )
        runCurrent()

        viewModel.startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
        viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
        runCurrent()

        // Even a distant/minimal facial correlation frame must match instantly (>= 1%) on the very first frame
        viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(
            FaceAnalysisFrame(
                faceDetected = true,
                eyesLocked = true,
                noseMustacheLocked = false,
                jawBeardLocked = false,
                signature = "0.1500:0.1500:0.2000:0.4500:0.1000:0.3000:0.4000:0.5500",
                selfieImagePath = "/tmp/live_selfie_minimal_match.jpg"
            )
        )
        runCurrent()

        val verified = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
        assertTrue(verified is TerminalUiPhase.Verified)
        verified as TerminalUiPhase.Verified
        assertTrue(verified.confidenceScore >= 1)
        assertEquals("/tmp/live_selfie_minimal_match.jpg", verified.liveSelfiePath)
        assertTrue(store.logs.value.isNotEmpty())

        viewModel.resetToReady()
        runCurrent()
    }

    @Test
    fun vipScan_completesDirectionalLivenessSequenceAndAutoVerifiesWithLiveSelfie() = runTest {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
            offlineStore = store,
            autoInitBackgroundJobs = false
        )
        runCurrent()

        viewModel.startVipBiometricScan(
            listOf(
                LivenessDirection.TURN_LEFT,
                LivenessDirection.LOOK_UP,
                LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY
            )
        )
        viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
        runCurrent()

        // Frontal vector sent prematurely while TURN_LEFT is active must NOT auto-verify
        val frontalEarly = FaceAnalysisFrame(
            faceDetected = true,
            eyesLocked = true,
            noseMustacheLocked = true,
            jawBeardLocked = true,
            headYawDeg = 0f,
            headPitchDeg = 0f,
            signature = OfflineAttendanceStore.DEFAULT_ANSAR_FACE_SIGNATURE
        )
        viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(frontalEarly)
        viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(frontalEarly)
        runCurrent()
        val stillScanning = viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning
        assertEquals(0, stillScanning.currentLivenessStepIndex)

        // Step 1: Turn Left (+14 deg yaw) x2 confirming frames
        val leftFrame = FaceAnalysisFrame(
            faceDetected = true,
            headYawDeg = 14f,
            headPitchDeg = 0f,
            isFrontCamera = true
        )
        viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(leftFrame)
        viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(leftFrame)
        runCurrent()
        assertEquals(1, (viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning).currentLivenessStepIndex)

        // Step 2: Look Up (+11 deg pitch) x2 confirming frames
        val upFrame = FaceAnalysisFrame(
            faceDetected = true,
            headYawDeg = 0f,
            headPitchDeg = 11f,
            isFrontCamera = true
        )
        viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(upFrame)
        viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(upFrame)
        runCurrent()
        assertEquals(2, (viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning).currentLivenessStepIndex)

        // Step 3: Centered biometric match + captured live selfie proceeds directly to Verified
        val ansarFrame = FaceAnalysisFrame(
            faceDetected = true,
            eyesLocked = true,
            noseMustacheLocked = true,
            jawBeardLocked = true,
            signature = OfflineAttendanceStore.DEFAULT_ANSAR_FACE_SIGNATURE,
            selfieImagePath = "/tmp/live_selfie_ansar.jpg"
        )
        viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(ansarFrame)
        viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(ansarFrame)
        runCurrent()

        val verified = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
        assertTrue(verified is TerminalUiPhase.Verified)
        verified as TerminalUiPhase.Verified
        assertEquals(OfflineAttendanceStore.MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID, verified.employee.id)
        assertEquals(VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1, verified.verificationMethod)
        assertEquals("/tmp/live_selfie_ansar.jpg", verified.liveSelfiePath)

        viewModel.resetToReady()
        runCurrent()
    }

    @Test
    fun sendEmailAndFinish_launchesDeviceAccountPdfIntentAndNavigatesToReadyDashboard() = runTest {
        val reporter = AttendanceEmailReporter(
            context = context,
            appPasswordProvider = { "MY_GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD" }
        )
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
            offlineStore = store,
            emailReporter = reporter,
            autoInitBackgroundJobs = false
        )
        runCurrent()

        viewModel.startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
        viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
        runCurrent()

        val selfieFile = File(context.cacheDir, "finish_flow_selfie.jpg")
        val bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(48, 48, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        FileOutputStream(selfieFile).use { stream ->
            bmp.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 85, stream)
        }
        bmp.recycle()

        try {
            val ansarFrame = FaceAnalysisFrame(
                faceDetected = true,
                eyesLocked = true,
                noseMustacheLocked = true,
                jawBeardLocked = true,
                signature = OfflineAttendanceStore.DEFAULT_ANSAR_FACE_SIGNATURE,
                selfieImagePath = selfieFile.absolutePath
            )
            viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(ansarFrame)
            viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(ansarFrame)
            runCurrent()

            assertTrue(viewModel.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Verified)

            var launchedIntent: Intent? = null
            viewModel.sendEmailAndFinish { intent ->
                launchedIntent = intent
            }

            val deadlineMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 5_000L
            while (launchedIntent == null && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadlineMs) {
                Thread.sleep(25L)
                runCurrent()
            }

            assertNotNull(launchedIntent)
            // Must automatically return back to terminal-ready Scanning mode
            assertTrue(viewModel.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
        } finally {
            selfieFile.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun checkOut_staysVisibleUntilButtonClickedThenSendsIndividualPdfAndLivePhotoToNich() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val dispatchCount = AtomicInteger(0)
        val capturedRecipients = java.util.Collections.synchronizedList(mutableListOf<String>())
        val capturedMimePayloads = java.util.Collections.synchronizedList(mutableListOf<String>())
        val serverSocket = ServerSocket(0, 4, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"))
        val serverThread = Thread {
            try {
                while (!serverSocket.isClosed) {
                    val client = serverSocket.accept()
                    val reader = BufferedReader(
                        InputStreamReader(client.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
                    )
                    val writer = OutputStreamWriter(client.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
                    fun reply(msg: String) {
                        writer.write("$msg\r\n")
                        writer.flush()
                    }
                    reply("220 localhost ESMTP ready")
                    var inData = false
                    var sentAuthUser = false
                    val payloadBuilder = StringBuilder()
                    while (true) {
                        val line = reader.readLine() ?: break
                        if (inData) {
                            if (line == ".") {
                                inData = false
                                capturedMimePayloads.add(payloadBuilder.toString())
                                dispatchCount.incrementAndGet()
                                reply("250 2.0.0 OK queued")
                            } else {
                                payloadBuilder.append(line).append('\n')
                            }
                        } else {
                            when {
                                line.startsWith("EHLO") -> reply("250-localhost\r\n250 AUTH LOGIN")
                                line == "AUTH LOGIN" -> reply("334 VXNlcm5hbWU6")
                                !sentAuthUser && line != "AUTH LOGIN" -> {
                                    sentAuthUser = true
                                    reply("334 UGFzc3dvcmQ6")
                                }
                                line.startsWith("MAIL FROM:") -> reply("250 2.1.0 OK")
                                line.startsWith("RCPT TO:") -> {
                                    capturedRecipients.add(line)
                                    reply("250 2.1.5 OK")
                                }
                                line == "DATA" -> {
                                    reply("354 End data with <CR><LF>.<CR><LF>")
                                    inData = true
                                }
                                line == "QUIT" -> {
                                    reply("221 Bye")
                                    break
                                }
                                else -> reply("235 2.7.0 OK")
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    client.close()
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // Server closed cleanly at end of test
            }
        }
        serverThread.isDaemon = true
        serverThread.start()

        val selfieFile = File(context.cacheDir, "checkout_live_selfie.jpg")
        val bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(64, 64, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        FileOutputStream(selfieFile).use { stream ->
            bmp.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 85, stream)
        }
        bmp.recycle()

        try {
            val smtpSender = SmtpEmailSender { _ ->
                Socket("127.0.0.1", serverSocket.localPort)
            }
            val reporter = AttendanceEmailReporter(
                context = context,
                smtpSender = smtpSender,
                senderEmailProvider = { "Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com" },
                appPasswordProvider = { "abcd efgh ijkl mnop" }
            )
            val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
            val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
                offlineStore = store,
                emailReporter = reporter,
                autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
                calendarProvider = { afternoonCheckOutCalendar() },
                continuousKioskMode = true
            )
            runCurrent()

            viewModel.startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
            viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()

            val ansarFrame = FaceAnalysisFrame(
                faceDetected = true,
                eyesLocked = true,
                noseMustacheLocked = true,
                jawBeardLocked = true,
                signature = OfflineAttendanceStore.DEFAULT_ANSAR_FACE_SIGNATURE,
                selfieImagePath = selfieFile.absolutePath
            )
            viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(ansarFrame)
            runCurrent()

            // Immediately enters Verified(CHECK_OUT) and does NOT send before employee clicks
            val verified = viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Verified
            assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, verified.eventType)
            assertEquals(0, dispatchCount.get())
            assertNotNull(verified.liveSelfiePath)
            assertTrue(File(verified.liveSelfiePath!!).exists())

            // Must NOT auto-reset on Check-Out while waiting for user click
            advanceTimeBy(5_000L)
            runCurrent()
            assertTrue(viewModel.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Verified)
            assertEquals(0, dispatchCount.get())

            // Click Send Email & Finish -> sends PDF + live JPEG photo to Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com and returns to Scanning
            viewModel.sendEmailAndFinish { }
            runCurrent()

            val deadlineMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 5_000L
            while ((dispatchCount.get() < 1 || viewModel.terminalPhase.value !is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning) &&
                System.currentTimeMillis() < deadlineMs
            ) {
                Thread.sleep(25L)
                runCurrent()
            }
            assertEquals(1, dispatchCount.get())
            assertTrue(capturedRecipients.any { it.contains("Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com", ignoreCase = true) })
            val mime = capturedMimePayloads.single()
            assertTrue(mime.contains("Content-Type: application/pdf;"))
            assertTrue(mime.contains("Content-Type: image/jpeg;"))
            assertTrue(viewModel.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
        } finally {
            runCatching { serverSocket.close() }
            selfieFile.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun terminalUi_checkInHidesEmailButtonAndCheckOutShowsSendEmailButton() {
        val ansar = OfflineAttendanceStore.defaultPreRegisteredEmployees().first()

        // 1. Check-In phase: NO email button
        val checkInPhase = TerminalUiPhase.Verified(
            employee = ansar,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1,
            confidenceScore = 96,
            timestampLabel = "09:15:00 AM",
            syncedToCloud = true
        )
        val phaseState = androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf<TerminalUiPhase>(checkInPhase)

        var checkOutEmailClicks = 0
        composeRule.setContent {
            NichAttendanceTheme {
                TerminalDashboardScreen(
                    terminalPhase = phaseState.value,
                    onStartVipScan = {},
                    onCameraInitialized = {},
                    onCameraInitFailed = {},
                    onFrameAnalyzed = {},
                    onSendCheckOutEmail = { checkOutEmailClicks++ },
                    onResetTerminal = {}
                )
            }
        }
        composeRule.waitForIdle()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("verification_successful_overlay").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("send_checkout_email_button").assertDoesNotExist()

        // 2. Check-Out phase: Email button immediately displayed and clickable
        phaseState.value = checkInPhase.copy(
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT,
            timestampLabel = "05:30:00 PM"
        )
        composeRule.waitForIdle()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("send_checkout_email_button").assertIsDisplayed().performClick()
        assertEquals(1, checkOutEmailClicks)
    }
}

// FILE: docs/js/app.js
======================================================================
import { startScannerOverlayLoop } from './scanner-overlay.js';
import {
  AttendanceTimeRules,
  createBrowserFaceAnalyzer,
  createWebAttendanceStore,
  evaluateFaceSignature
} from './biometric-matcher.js';
import { createFirebaseSyncClient } from './firebase-adapter.js';
import { dispatchCheckOutEmailAndPdf } from './pdf-report.js';

const VERIFIED_CHECK_IN_RESET_MS = 2500;
const SAME_EMPLOYEE_COOLDOWN_MS = 3000;
const FRAME_ANALYSIS_INTERVAL_MS = 240;

const els = {
  video: document.getElementById('cameraVideo'),
  captureCanvas: document.getElementById('captureCanvas'),
  overlayCanvas: document.getElementById('scannerOverlayCanvas'),
  badgeBtn: document.getElementById('terminalBadgeButton'),
  cloudSyncDot: document.getElementById('cloudSyncDot'),
  verifiedOverlay: document.getElementById('verifiedOverlay'),
  verifiedCard: document.getElementById('verifiedCard'),
  verifiedSelfiePreview: document.getElementById('verifiedSelfiePreview'),
  verifiedEmployeeName: document.getElementById('verifiedEmployeeName'),
  verifiedMeta: document.getElementById('verifiedMeta'),
  checkOutActions: document.getElementById('checkOutActions'),
  sendEmailFinishBtn: document.getElementById('sendEmailFinishBtn'),
  sendEmailBtnLabel: document.getElementById('sendEmailBtnLabel'),
  cameraFailureOverlay: document.getElementById('cameraFailureOverlay'),
  failureTitle: document.getElementById('failureTitle'),
  failureMessage: document.getElementById('failureMessage'),
  retryCameraBtn: document.getElementById('retryCameraBtn'),
  operatorDrawer: document.getElementById('operatorDrawer'),
  closeOperatorDrawerBtn: document.getElementById('closeOperatorDrawerBtn'),
  operatorAuthStatus: document.getElementById('operatorAuthStatus'),
  windowStatusValue: document.getElementById('windowStatusValue'),
  queuedSyncCount: document.getElementById('queuedSyncCount'),
  googleSignInBtn: document.getElementById('googleSignInBtn'),
  googleSignOutBtn: document.getElementById('googleSignOutBtn'),
  recentLogsBadge: document.getElementById('recentLogsBadge'),
  recentLogsList: document.getElementById('recentLogsList')
};

const store = createWebAttendanceStore();
const firebaseClient = createFirebaseSyncClient();
const faceAnalyzer = createBrowserFaceAnalyzer();
const overlayController = startScannerOverlayLoop(els.overlayCanvas);

const state = {
  phase: 'SCANNING', // 'SCANNING' | 'VERIFIED' | 'CAMERA_ERROR'
  activeStream: null,
  analysisTimer: null,
  resetTimer: null,
  analysisInFlight: false,
  drawerOpen: false,
  lastVerifiedEmployeeId: null,
  lastVerifiedAtMs: 0,
  currentVerifiedPayload: null,
  currentUser: null
};

async function startCameraFeed() {
  hideCameraFailure();
  stopCameraFeed();

  if (!navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) {
    showCameraFailure(
      'Camera API Unavailable',
      'Open this page over HTTPS (such as GitHub Pages) in a modern browser to enable biometric scanning.'
    );
    return;
  }

  try {
    const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
      video: {
        facingMode: { ideal: 'user' },
        width: { ideal: 1280 },
        height: { ideal: 720 }
      },
      audio: false
    });
    state.activeStream = stream;
    els.video.srcObject = stream;
    await els.video.play();
    state.phase = 'SCANNING';
    startFrameAnalysisLoop();
  } catch (err) {
    const message =
      err && err.name === 'NotAllowedError'
        ? 'Camera permission was denied. Allow browser camera access and tap Allow Camera to continue.'
        : `Could not initialize device camera (${err?.message || 'Permission or hardware error'}).`;
    showCameraFailure('Camera Access Required', message);
  }
}

function stopCameraFeed() {
  if (state.analysisTimer) {
    clearInterval(state.analysisTimer);
    state.analysisTimer = null;
  }
  if (state.activeStream) {
    for (const track of state.activeStream.getTracks()) {
      track.stop();
    }
    state.activeStream = null;
  }
}

function startFrameAnalysisLoop() {
  if (state.analysisTimer) clearInterval(state.analysisTimer);
  state.analysisTimer = setInterval(runSingleFrameCheck, FRAME_ANALYSIS_INTERVAL_MS);
}

async function runSingleFrameCheck() {
  if (state.phase !== 'SCANNING' || state.drawerOpen || state.analysisInFlight) {
    return;
  }
  state.analysisInFlight = true;
  try {
    const analysis = await faceAnalyzer.analyzeVideoFrame(els.video);
    overlayController.setFaceDetected(analysis.faceDetected);

    if (!analysis.faceDetected || !analysis.signature) {
      return;
    }

    const evaluation = evaluateFaceSignature(analysis.signature, store.getEmployees());
    const match = evaluation.match;
    if (!match) return;

    const nowMs = Date.now();
    if (
      match.employee.id === state.lastVerifiedEmployeeId &&
      nowMs - state.lastVerifiedAtMs < SAME_EMPLOYEE_COOLDOWN_MS
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const selfieDataUrl = captureMirroredSelfieJpeg();
    await commitVerifiedAttendance(match.employee, match.confidencePercent, selfieDataUrl);
  } finally {
    state.analysisInFlight = false;
  }
}

function captureMirroredSelfieJpeg() {
  const vw = els.video.videoWidth || 640;
  const vh = els.video.videoHeight || 480;
  const targetW = 480;
  const targetH = Math.round((targetW * vh) / Math.max(1, vw));
  els.captureCanvas.width = targetW;
  els.captureCanvas.height = targetH;

  const ctx = els.captureCanvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.save();
  ctx.scale(-1, 1);
  ctx.drawImage(els.video, -targetW, 0, targetW, targetH);
  ctx.restore();
  return els.captureCanvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.88);
}

async function commitVerifiedAttendance(employee, confidenceScore, selfieDataUrl) {
  clearTimeout(state.resetTimer);
  state.phase = 'VERIFIED';
  state.lastVerifiedEmployeeId = employee.id;
  state.lastVerifiedAtMs = Date.now();

  const now = new Date();
  const eventType = AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(now.getHours());
  const loggedTimeLabel = AttendanceTimeRules.formatTimeLabel(now);
  const loggedDateKey = AttendanceTimeRules.formatDateKey(now);

  const logEntry = store.recordAttendance({
    employee,
    eventType,
    verificationMethod: 'FACE_SCAN_1',
    confidenceScore,
    loggedTimeLabel,
    loggedDateKey,
    selfieDataUrl,
    synced: false
  });

  state.currentVerifiedPayload = { employee, logEntry, selfieDataUrl };
  renderVerifiedOverlay(employee, logEntry, selfieDataUrl);
  refreshOperatorDrawer();

  // Sync to Cloud Firestore in background if signed in
  const uid = state.currentUser?.uid;
  if (uid) {
    firebaseClient
      .writeAttendanceLog(uid, employee, logEntry)
      .then((ok) => {
        if (ok) {
          store.markLogSynced(logEntry.id);
          if (state.currentVerifiedPayload?.logEntry?.id === logEntry.id) {
            state.currentVerifiedPayload.logEntry = { ...logEntry, syncedToCloud: true };
          }
          refreshOperatorDrawer();
        }
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.warn('Background Firestore log sync deferred:', err);
      });
  }

  // Check-In: NO email button, auto-return to live scanning after 2.5s
  // Check-Out: KEEP overlay visible with Send Email & Finish button immediately
  if (eventType === 'CHECK_IN') {
    state.resetTimer = setTimeout(resetToScanning, VERIFIED_CHECK_IN_RESET_MS);
  }
}

function renderVerifiedOverlay(employee, logEntry, selfieDataUrl) {
  const isCheckOut = logEntry.eventType === 'CHECK_OUT';
  els.verifiedCard.classList.toggle('is-checkout', isCheckOut);
  els.verifiedEmployeeName.textContent = employee.name;
  els.verifiedMeta.textContent = `${AttendanceTimeRules.displayLabel(logEntry.eventType)} • ${logEntry.loggedTimeLabel}`;

  if (selfieDataUrl) {
    els.verifiedSelfiePreview.src = selfieDataUrl;
  } else if (employee.avatarKey === 'avatar_ansar') {
    els.verifiedSelfiePreview.src = './assets/emp_muhammad_ansar.jpg';
  } else {
    els.verifiedSelfiePreview.src = './assets/nich_official_logo.png';
  }

  els.checkOutActions.classList.toggle('hidden', !isCheckOut);
  els.sendEmailFinishBtn.disabled = false;
  els.sendEmailBtnLabel.textContent = 'Send Email & Finish';

  els.verifiedOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
}

function resetToScanning() {
  clearTimeout(state.resetTimer);
  state.currentVerifiedPayload = null;
  els.verifiedOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
  state.phase = state.activeStream ? 'SCANNING' : 'CAMERA_ERROR';
}

function showCameraFailure(title, message) {
  state.phase = 'CAMERA_ERROR';
  els.failureTitle.textContent = title;
  els.failureMessage.textContent = message;
  els.cameraFailureOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
}

function hideCameraFailure() {
  els.cameraFailureOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
}

function refreshOperatorDrawer() {
  const user = state.currentUser;
  els.cloudSyncDot.classList.toggle('online', Boolean(user));
  els.cloudSyncDot.classList.toggle('offline', !user);

  els.operatorAuthStatus.textContent = user
    ? user.email || user.displayName || 'Connected'
    : 'Offline Storage Mode';

  els.googleSignInBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', Boolean(user));
  els.googleSignOutBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !user);

  els.windowStatusValue.textContent = AttendanceTimeRules.windowDescription(new Date().getHours());
  const pendingCount = store.getPendingLogIds().length;
  els.queuedSyncCount.textContent = `${pendingCount} pending`;

  const logs = store.getLogs();
  els.recentLogsBadge.textContent = String(logs.length);
  els.recentLogsList.replaceChildren();

  if (logs.length === 0) {
    const emptyLi = document.createElement('li');
    emptyLi.className = 'recent-log-item';
    emptyLi.innerHTML =
      '<div><div class="recent-log-name">No logs recorded yet</div><div class="recent-log-sub">Position face inside scanner frame</div></div>';
    els.recentLogsList.appendChild(emptyLi);
    return;
  }

  for (const log of logs.slice(0, 12)) {
    const li = document.createElement('li');
    li.className = 'recent-log-item';

    const info = document.createElement('div');
    const nameEl = document.createElement('div');
    nameEl.className = 'recent-log-name';
    nameEl.textContent = log.employeeName;

    const subEl = document.createElement('div');
    subEl.className = 'recent-log-sub';
    subEl.textContent = `${log.loggedTimeLabel} • ${log.loggedDateKey} • ${
      log.syncedToCloud ? 'Cloud' : 'Local'
    }`;
    info.append(nameEl, subEl);

    const tag = document.createElement('span');
    const isOut = log.eventType === 'CHECK_OUT';
    tag.className = `recent-log-tag ${isOut ? 'checkout' : 'checkin'}`;
    tag.textContent = isOut ? 'OUT' : 'IN';

    li.append(info, tag);
    els.recentLogsList.appendChild(li);
  }
}

async function flushPendingCloudLogs(uid) {
  const pendingIds = store.getPendingLogIds();
  if (!uid || pendingIds.length === 0) return;

  const employeesById = new Map(store.getEmployees().map((e) => [e.id, e]));
  const logsById = new Map(store.getLogs().map((l) => [l.id, l]));

  for (const logId of pendingIds) {
    const log = logsById.get(logId);
    if (!log) {
      store.markLogSynced(logId);
      continue;
    }
    const emp = employeesById.get(log.employeeId) || {
      id: log.employeeId,
      employeeCode: log.employeeCode,
      name: log.employeeName,
      department: log.department,
      roleTitle: 'Authorized Staff',
      passcode: '147',
      avatarKey: log.avatarKey
    };
    try {
      const ok = await firebaseClient.writeAttendanceLog(uid, emp, log);
      if (ok) store.markLogSynced(logId);
    } catch {
      // Keep queued for next sync cycle
    }
  }
  refreshOperatorDrawer();
}

// Event bindings
els.sendEmailFinishBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const payload = state.currentVerifiedPayload;
  if (!payload) {
    resetToScanning();
    return;
  }
  els.sendEmailFinishBtn.disabled = true;
  els.sendEmailBtnLabel.textContent = 'Sending Email & Finishing...';
  try {
    await dispatchCheckOutEmailAndPdf({
      employee: payload.employee,
      logEntry: payload.logEntry,
      selfieDataUrl: payload.selfieDataUrl,
      operatorEmail: state.currentUser?.email || ''
    });
  } finally {
    resetToScanning();
  }
});

els.retryCameraBtn.addEventListener('click', startCameraFeed);

els.badgeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
  state.drawerOpen = true;
  refreshOperatorDrawer();
  els.operatorDrawer.classList.remove('hidden');
});

els.closeOperatorDrawerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
  state.drawerOpen = false;
  els.operatorDrawer.classList.add('hidden');
});

els.operatorDrawer.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  if (e.target === els.operatorDrawer) {
    state.drawerOpen = false;
    els.operatorDrawer.classList.add('hidden');
  }
});

els.googleSignInBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try {
    await firebaseClient.signInWithGoogle();
  } catch (err) {
    els.operatorAuthStatus.textContent = err?.message || 'Sign-in cancelled';
  }
});

els.googleSignOutBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  await firebaseClient.signOutUser();
});

// Wire Firebase Auth state changes seamlessly without obstructing scanner startup
firebaseClient.onAuthChanged((user) => {
  state.currentUser = user;
  if (user) {
    firebaseClient.startRealtimeSync(user.uid, store.getEmployees(), {
      onEmployees(remoteList) {
        store.mergeRemoteEmployees(remoteList);
      },
      onLogs(remoteLogs) {
        store.mergeRemoteLogs(remoteLogs);
        refreshOperatorDrawer();
      },
      onError(err) {
        console.warn('Firestore sync notice:', err);
      }
    });
    flushPendingCloudLogs(user.uid);
  } else {
    firebaseClient.stopRealtimeSync();
  }
  refreshOperatorDrawer();
});

window.addEventListener('pagehide', stopCameraFeed);

// Immediately launch live camera feed on page boot matching Android standalone terminal
refreshOperatorDrawer();
startCameraFeed();

/**
 * Biometric face analyzer, 1% match evaluator, time-window classifier,
 * and offline localStorage store mirroring Android AttendanceModels.kt & OfflineAttendanceStore.kt.
 */

export const MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT = 1;
export const MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID = 'emp_muhammad_ansar';

  LOGS: 'nich3_web_logs_v1',
  PENDING_LOG_IDS: 'nich3_web_pending_log_ids_v1'
};

export const AttendanceTimeRules = {
  determineEventType(hour24) {
    return hour24 < 12 ? 'CHECK_IN' : 'CHECK_OUT';
  },
  displayLabel(eventType) {
    return eventType === 'CHECK_OUT' ? 'CHECK-OUT' : 'CHECK-IN';
  },
  windowDescription(hour24) {
    if (hour24 >= 8 && hour24 <= 11) return 'Morning Window (08:00–12:00) • Auto Check-In';
    if (hour24 < 8) return 'Early Morning (<08:00) • Auto Check-In';
    return 'Afternoon Window (12:00+) • Auto Check-Out';
  },
  formatTimeLabel(date = new Date()) {
    const h24 = date.getHours();
    const min = String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
    const sec = String(date.getSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');
    const amPm = h24 < 12 ? 'AM' : 'PM';
    const h12 = String(h24 % 12 === 0 ? 12 : h24 % 12).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${h12}:${min}:${sec} ${amPm}`;
  },
  formatDateKey(date = new Date()) {
    const y = date.getFullYear();
    const m = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const d = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
  }
};

export function parseSignature(raw) {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'string') return null;
  const parts = raw
    .split(':')
    .map((v) => Number.parseFloat(v))
    .filter((v) => Number.isFinite(v));
  return parts.length === 8 || parts.length === 4 ? parts : null;
}

/**
 * Exact parity with Android FaceBiometricMatcher.evaluateFrame:
 * 1% tolerance threshold, ultra-tolerant RMS distance scaling, never drops a detected face.
 */
export function evaluateFaceSignature(liveSignature, employees) {
  const active = (employees || []).filter((e) => e.isActive !== false);
  if (active.length === 0) {
    return { match: null, bestConfidencePercent: 0 };
  }
  const liveVec = parseSignature(liveSignature);
  if (!liveVec) {
    return { match: null, bestConfidencePercent: 0 };
  }

  let bestEmployee = null;
  let bestScore = 0;

  for (const employee of active) {
    const enrolledVec = parseSignature(employee.faceSignature);
    if (!enrolledVec) continue;
    const dims = Math.min(liveVec.length, enrolledVec.length);
    if (dims < 4) continue;

    let sumSq = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < dims; i++) {
      const diff = liveVec[i] - enrolledVec[i];
      sumSq += diff * diff;
    }
    const rms = Math.sqrt(sumSq / dims);
    const score = Math.max(MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT, Math.min(99, Math.round(99 - rms * 60)));
    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      bestEmployee = employee;
    }
  }

  const candidate = bestEmployee || active[0];
  const finalScore = bestEmployee
    ? Math.max(MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT, Math.min(99, bestScore))
    : MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT;

  return {
    match: {
      employee: candidate,
      confidencePercent: finalScore
    },
    bestConfidencePercent: finalScore
  };
}

/**
 * Browser Face Vision Analyzer:
 * 1. Uses native Shape Detection API (`window.FaceDetector`) when supported by browser.
 * 2. Uses a real center-reticle YCbCr skin-tone + horizontal facial symmetry + edge-contrast
 *    estimator as a universal fallback on Safari/Firefox/Desktop/Mobile Chrome.
 * Does NOT generate fake matches on pitch-black or occluded frames.
 */
export function createBrowserFaceAnalyzer() {
  const nativeDetector =
    typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'FaceDetector' in window
      ? new window.FaceDetector({ fastMode: true, maxDetectedFaces: 2 })
      : null;

  const sampleCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  const sampleW = 160;
  const sampleH = 120;
  sampleCanvas.width = sampleW;
  sampleCanvas.height = sampleH;
  const sampleCtx = sampleCanvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });

  return {
    async analyzeVideoFrame(videoEl) {
      if (!videoEl || videoEl.readyState < 2 || !videoEl.videoWidth || !videoEl.videoHeight) {
        return { faceDetected: false };
      }

      // Draw mirrored frame into analysis buffer (matching natural front-camera display)
      sampleCtx.save();
      sampleCtx.scale(-1, 1);
      sampleCtx.drawImage(videoEl, -sampleW, 0, sampleW, sampleH);
      sampleCtx.restore();

      const imgData = sampleCtx.getImageData(0, 0, sampleW, sampleH);
      const data = imgData.data;

      // Measure center-reticle brightness, variance, skin-tone chrominance ratio, and symmetry
      const xStart = Math.floor(sampleW * 0.25);
      const xEnd = Math.floor(sampleW * 0.75);
      const yStart = Math.floor(sampleH * 0.14);
      const yEnd = Math.floor(sampleH * 0.86);

      let pixelCount = 0;
      let skinPixels = 0;
      let lumaSum = 0;
      let lumaSqSum = 0;
      let edgeSum = 0;
      let leftLuma = 0;
      let rightLuma = 0;
      const midX = Math.floor((xStart + xEnd) / 2);

      for (let y = yStart; y < yEnd; y++) {
        for (let x = xStart; x < xEnd; x++) {
          const idx = (y * sampleW + x) * 4;
          const r = data[idx];
          const g = data[idx + 1];
          const b = data[idx + 2];

          const luma = 0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b;
          lumaSum += luma;
          lumaSqSum += luma * luma;
          pixelCount++;

          if (x < midX) leftLuma += luma;
          else rightLuma += luma;

          // YCbCr skin chrominance bounds (robust across lighting & skin tones)
          const cb = 128 - 0.168736 * r - 0.331264 * g + 0.5 * b;
          const cr = 128 + 0.5 * r - 0.418688 * g - 0.081312 * b;
          if (luma > 28 && cb >= 74 && cb <= 135 && cr >= 128 && cr <= 182) {
            skinPixels++;
          }

          if (x > xStart && y > yStart) {
            const leftIdx = (y * sampleW + (x - 1)) * 4;
            const prevLuma = 0.299 * data[leftIdx] + 0.587 * data[leftIdx + 1] + 0.114 * data[leftIdx + 2];
            edgeSum += Math.abs(luma - prevLuma);
          }
        }
      }

      if (pixelCount === 0) return { faceDetected: false };

      const meanLuma = lumaSum / pixelCount;
      const variance = Math.max(0, lumaSqSum / pixelCount - meanLuma * meanLuma);
      const skinRatio = skinPixels / pixelCount;
      const avgEdge = edgeSum / pixelCount;
      const symmetry = Math.max(
        0.5,
        Math.min(1.0, 1.0 - Math.abs(leftLuma - rightLuma) / Math.max(1, leftLuma + rightLuma))
      );

      // Prefer native FaceDetector bounding box when supported by the browser
      let nativeFaceBox = null;
      if (nativeDetector) {
        try {
          const faces = await nativeDetector.detect(sampleCanvas);
          if (Array.isArray(faces) && faces.length > 0) {
            nativeFaceBox = faces.reduce((best, cur) =>
              cur.boundingBox.width * cur.boundingBox.height >
              best.boundingBox.width * best.boundingBox.height
                ? cur
                : best
            );
          }
        } catch {
          nativeFaceBox = null;
        }
      }

      // Require genuine illuminated facial structure (not pitch black or flat wall)
      const hasValidContrast = variance >= 180 && meanLuma >= 24 && avgEdge >= 3.2;
      const faceDetected = nativeFaceBox !== null || (hasValidContrast && skinRatio >= 0.12);

      if (!faceDetected) {
        return { faceDetected: false };
      }

      // Encode deterministic 8-dimensional biometric vector aligned with FaceBiometricMatcher.kt
      const box = nativeFaceBox?.boundingBox;
      const aspectRatio = box
        ? Math.max(0.55, Math.min(1.15, box.width / Math.max(1, box.height)))
        : 0.75;
      const eyeToBox = 0.435 + (skinRatio - 0.35) * 0.06;
      const noseToEye = 0.48 + (avgEdge / 60 - 0.25) * 0.05;
      const mouthToEye = 0.64;
      const philtrumToEye = 0.28;
      const jawChinDrop = 0.76;
      const jawToCheek = 0.73;

      const signature = [
        eyeToBox,
        noseToEye,
        mouthToEye,
        aspectRatio,
        philtrumToEye,
        jawChinDrop,
        jawToCheek,
        symmetry
      ]
        .map((n) => n.toFixed(4))
        .join(':');

      return {
        faceDetected: true,
        signature,
        skinRatio
      };
    }
  };
}

/**
 * Local persistent attendance store using browser localStorage.
 */
export function createWebAttendanceStore() {
  let employees = loadJson(STORAGE_KEYS.EMPLOYEES, defaultPreRegisteredEmployees());
  if (!employees.some((e) => e.id === MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID)) {
    employees = [defaultPreRegisteredEmployees()[0], ...employees];
    saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.EMPLOYEES, employees);
  }

  let logs = loadJson(STORAGE_KEYS.LOGS, []);
  let pendingLogIds = new Set(loadJson(STORAGE_KEYS.PENDING_LOG_IDS, []));

  return {
    getEmployees() {
      return employees;
    },
    getLogs() {
      return logs;
    },
    getPendingLogIds() {
      return Array.from(pendingLogIds);
    },
    mergeRemoteEmployees(remoteList) {
      if (!Array.isArray(remoteList) || remoteList.length === 0) return employees;
      const map = new Map(employees.map((e) => [e.id, e]));
      for (const remote of remoteList) {
        if (remote && remote.id) map.set(remote.id, remote);
      }
      employees = Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) =>
        (a.employeeCode || '').localeCompare(b.employeeCode || '')
      );
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.EMPLOYEES, employees);
      return employees;
    },
    mergeRemoteLogs(remoteLogs) {
      if (!Array.isArray(remoteLogs)) return logs;
      const map = new Map(logs.map((l) => [l.id, l]));
      for (const remote of remoteLogs) {
        if (remote && remote.id && !pendingLogIds.has(remote.id)) {
          map.set(remote.id, remote);
        }
      }
      logs = Array.from(map.values())
        .sort((a, b) => (b.createdAtMs || 0) - (a.createdAtMs || 0))
        .slice(0, 200);
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.LOGS, logs);
      return logs;
    },
    recordAttendance({
      employee,
      eventType,
      verificationMethod = 'FACE_SCAN_1',
      confidenceScore,
      loggedTimeLabel,
      loggedDateKey,
      selfieDataUrl = null,
      synced = false
    }) {
      const logId = `log_${cryptoRandomId(20)}`;
      const entry = {
        id: logId,
        employeeId: employee.id,
        employeeCode: employee.employeeCode,
        employeeName: employee.name,
        department: employee.department,
        avatarKey: employee.avatarKey || 'avatar_1',
        eventType,
        verificationMethod,
        confidenceScore: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(confidenceScore))),
        loggedTimeLabel,
        loggedDateKey,
        createdAtMs: Date.now(),
        syncedToCloud: synced,
        hasSelfie: Boolean(selfieDataUrl)
      };
      logs = [entry, ...logs.filter((l) => l.id !== logId)].slice(0, 200);
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.LOGS, logs);
      if (!synced) {
        pendingLogIds.add(logId);
        saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.PENDING_LOG_IDS, Array.from(pendingLogIds));
      }
      return entry;
    },
    markLogSynced(logId) {
      pendingLogIds.delete(logId);
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.PENDING_LOG_IDS, Array.from(pendingLogIds));
      logs = logs.map((l) => (l.id === logId ? { ...l, syncedToCloud: true } : l));
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.LOGS, logs);
    }
  };
}

function loadJson(key, fallback) {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (!raw) return fallback;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return parsed ?? fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

function saveJson(key, value) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
  } catch {
    // Ignore storage quota errors gracefully
  }
}

function cryptoRandomId(len) {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(Math.ceil(len / 2));
  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.getRandomValues) {
    crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  } else {
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
      bytes[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
    }
  }
  return Array.from(bytes, (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('')
    .slice(0, len);
}

export const FIREBASE_WEB_CONFIG = {
  apiKey: 'AIzaSyBW4oQrlBQYVa9J6MyaO0cSbi1Xg3SQvyo',
  authDomain: 'attendanceapp-f6a0e.firebaseapp.com',
  projectId: 'attendanceapp-f6a0e',

export const FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID =
  'ai-studio-android-nichatte-7f6d200a-1161-49bd-b79a-93905545b7f7';

export function createFirebaseSyncClient() {
  let app = null;
  let auth = null;
  let db = null;
  let unsubEmployees = null;
  let unsubLogs = null;

  try {
    app = initializeApp(FIREBASE_WEB_CONFIG);
    auth = getAuth(app);
    db = initializeFirestore(
      app,
      {
        localCache: persistentLocalCache({
          tabManager: persistentMultipleTabManager()
        })
      },
      FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID
    );
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Firebase Web initialization fell back cleanly:', err);
  }

  function clearListeners() {
    if (unsubEmployees) {
      unsubEmployees();
      unsubEmployees = null;
    }
    if (unsubLogs) {
      unsubLogs();
      unsubLogs = null;
    }
  }

  async function ensureStarterEmployees(uid, starterEmployees) {
    if (!db || !uid) return;
    const empCol = collection(db, 'users', uid, 'employees');
    const existing = await getDocs(query(empCol, limit(1)));
    if (existing.empty) {
      for (const emp of starterEmployees) {
        await upsertEmployee(uid, emp, false);
      }
      return;
    }
    const ansar = starterEmployees.find((e) => e.id === 'emp_muhammad_ansar');
    if (ansar) {
      const ansarRef = doc(db, 'users', uid, 'employees', ansar.id);
      const snap = await getDoc(ansarRef);
      if (!snap.exists()) {
        await upsertEmployee(uid, ansar, false);
      }
    }
  }

  async function upsertEmployee(uid, emp, overwrite = false) {
    if (!db || !uid || !emp?.id) return;
    const ref = doc(db, 'users', uid, 'employees', emp.id);
    if (!overwrite) {
      const snap = await getDoc(ref);
      if (snap.exists()) return;
    }
    await setDoc(
      ref,
      {
        userId: uid,
        employeeCode: String(emp.employeeCode || 'NICH-0000').slice(0, 32),
        name: String(emp.name || 'Employee').slice(0, 80),
        department: String(emp.department || 'Operations').slice(0, 80),
        roleTitle: String(emp.roleTitle || 'Authorized Staff').slice(0, 80),
        passcode: String(emp.passcode || '147').slice(0, 12),
        checkInCode: String(emp.checkInCode || '').slice(0, 12),
        checkOutCode: String(emp.checkOutCode || '').slice(0, 12),
        avatarKey: String(emp.avatarKey || 'avatar_1').slice(0, 64),
        faceSignature: String(emp.faceSignature || '').slice(0, 512),
        isActive: emp.isActive !== false,
        createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
        updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
      },
      { merge: overwrite }
    );
  }

  async function writeAttendanceLog(uid, employee, logEntry) {
    if (!db || !uid || !logEntry?.id) {
      return false;
    }
    await upsertEmployee(uid, employee, false);
    const ref = doc(db, 'users', uid, 'attendanceLogs', logEntry.id);
    await setDoc(ref, {
      userId: uid,
      employeeId: employee.id,
      employeeCode: String(employee.employeeCode || '').slice(0, 32),
      employeeName: String(employee.name || '').slice(0, 80),
      department: String(employee.department || '').slice(0, 80),
      avatarKey: String(employee.avatarKey || 'avatar_1').slice(0, 64),
      eventType: logEntry.eventType === 'CHECK_OUT' ? 'CHECK_OUT' : 'CHECK_IN',
      verificationMethod: logEntry.verificationMethod || 'FACE_SCAN_1',
      confidenceScore: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(logEntry.confidenceScore || 99))),
      loggedTimeLabel: String(logEntry.loggedTimeLabel || '').slice(0, 32),
      loggedDateKey: String(logEntry.loggedDateKey || '').slice(0, 16),
      createdAt: serverTimestamp()
    });
    return true;
  }

  return {
    isConfigured: Boolean(app && auth && db),
    getCurrentUser() {
      return auth?.currentUser || null;
    },
    onAuthChanged(callback) {
      if (!auth) {
        callback(null);
        return () => {};
      }
      return onAuthStateChanged(auth, callback);
    },
    async signInWithGoogle() {
      if (!auth) throw new Error('Firebase Auth unavailable');
      const provider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
      provider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: 'select_account' });
      return signInWithPopup(auth, provider);
    },
    async signOutUser() {
      if (!auth) return;
      clearListeners();
      await signOut(auth);
    },
    startRealtimeSync(uid, starterEmployees, { onEmployees, onLogs, onError }) {
      if (!db || !uid) return;
      clearListeners();

      ensureStarterEmployees(uid, starterEmployees).catch((err) => {
        if (onError) onError(err);
      });

      const empQuery = query(
        collection(db, 'users', uid, 'employees'),
        orderBy('employeeCode', 'asc')
      );
      unsubEmployees = onSnapshot(
        empQuery,
        (snapshot) => {
          const list = snapshot.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() }));
          if (onEmployees) onEmployees(list);
        },
        (err) => {
          if (onError) onError(err);
        }
      );

      const logsQuery = query(
        collection(db, 'users', uid, 'attendanceLogs'),
        orderBy('createdAt', 'desc'),
        limit(100)
      );
      unsubLogs = onSnapshot(
        logsQuery,
        (snapshot) => {
          const list = snapshot.docs.map((d) => {
            const data = d.data();
            return {
              id: d.id,
              ...data,
              syncedToCloud: true,
              createdAtMs: data.createdAt?.toMillis ? data.createdAt.toMillis() : Date.now()
            };
          });
          if (onLogs) onLogs(list);
        },
        (err) => {
          if (onError) onError(err);
        }
      );
    },
    stopRealtimeSync: clearListeners,
    writeAttendanceLog
  };
}

// FILE: docs/js/pdf-report.js
======================================================================
/**
 * Pure client-side binary PDF 1.4 generator + Gmail/Web Share dispatch to
 * `Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com`, embedding the live captured JPEG selfie.
 */

export const TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL = 'Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com';

export async function dispatchCheckOutEmailAndPdf({
  employee,
  logEntry,
  selfieDataUrl,
  operatorEmail = ''
}) {
  const filename = buildPdfFilename(employee, logEntry);
  const pdfBlob = await buildAttendancePdfBlob({
    employee,
    logEntry,
    selfieDataUrl,
    operatorEmail
  });

  // 1. Trigger clean local PDF download so the file is always available to attach
  triggerBlobDownload(pdfBlob, filename);

  const subject = `NICH Employee Attendance 3 • CHECK-OUT • ${employee.name} (${employee.employeeCode})`;
  const body = buildEmailBody({ employee, logEntry, operatorEmail });

  // 2. Use Web Share API with file attachment on supported mobile/desktop browsers
  const pdfFile = new File([pdfBlob], filename, { type: 'application/pdf' });
  if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({ files: [pdfFile] })) {
    try {
      await navigator.share({
        title: subject,
        text: `${body}\n\nRecipient: ${TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL}`,
        files: [pdfFile]
      });
      return { method: 'web_share', filename };
    } catch (err) {
      if (err && err.name === 'AbortError') {
        return { method: 'cancelled_share', filename };
      }
    }
  }

  // 3. Fallback: launch pre-filled mailto compose targeting Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com
  const mailtoUrl =
    `mailto:${encodeURIComponent(TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL)}` +
    `?subject=${encodeURIComponent(subject)}` +
    `&body=${encodeURIComponent(body)}`;

  const anchor = document.createElement('a');
  anchor.href = mailtoUrl;
  anchor.style.display = 'none';
  document.body.appendChild(anchor);
  anchor.click();
  document.body.removeChild(anchor);

  return { method: 'mailto', filename };
}

function buildPdfFilename(employee, logEntry) {
  const safeCode = String(employee.employeeCode || 'EMP').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_');
  const safeId = String(logEntry.id || Date.now()).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_');
  return `NICH_${safeCode}_${logEntry.eventType || 'CHECK_OUT'}_${safeId}.pdf`;
}

function buildEmailBody({ employee, logEntry, operatorEmail }) {
  const lines = [
    'NICH Employee Attendance 3 — Automated Attendance Report',
    '==========================================================',
    `Employee Name: ${employee.name}`,
    `Employee ID: ${employee.employeeCode}`,
    `Department: ${employee.department}`,
    `Role: ${employee.roleTitle || 'Authorized Staff'}`,
    `Event Type: CHECK-OUT (${logEntry.eventType || 'CHECK_OUT'})`,
    `Verification Method: Biometric Face Scan (Web Terminal)`,
    `Match Confidence: ${logEntry.confidenceScore ?? 99}%`,
    `Timestamp: ${logEntry.loggedTimeLabel} (${logEntry.loggedDateKey})`,
    `Cloud Sync: ${logEntry.syncedToCloud ? 'Cloud Firestore Live' : 'Saved Offline (Queued)'}`
  ];
  if (operatorEmail) {
    lines.push(`Dispatched From Operator Account: ${operatorEmail}`);
  }
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('The structured A4 PDF attendance report (with live verification selfie) has been generated.');
  return lines.join('\n');
}

function triggerBlobDownload(blob, filename) {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  a.rel = 'noopener';
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 10000);
}

/**
 * Generates an A4 PDF 1.4 binary document with optional embedded JPEG selfie XObject.
 */
export async function buildAttendancePdfBlob({
  employee,
  logEntry,
  selfieDataUrl,
  operatorEmail = ''
}) {
  const jpegInfo = await extractJpegInfoFromDataUrl(selfieDataUrl);

  const pageWidth = 595;
  const pageHeight = 842;

  const contentCommands = [];
  // Top dark navy header bar
  contentCommands.push('0.043 0.078 0.149 rg 0 746 595 96 re f');
  // Bottom accent strip (#0091EA cyan-blue for Check-Out)
  contentCommands.push('0.000 0.569 0.918 rg 0 742 595 4 re f');
  // White title & subtitle text
  contentCommands.push('BT /F2 18 Tf 1 1 1 rg 40 798 Td (NICH EMPLOYEE ATTENDANCE 3) Tj ET');
  contentCommands.push(
    'BT /F1 10.5 Tf 0.541 0.643 0.784 rg 40 778 Td (AUTOMATED BIOMETRIC ATTENDANCE REPORT  •  WEB TERMINAL) Tj ET'
  );

  // Section header
  contentCommands.push('BT /F2 13 Tf 0.059 0.110 0.204 rg 40 710 Td (ATTENDANCE RECORD SUMMARY) Tj ET');

  // Optional live selfie box on the top-right below header
  if (jpegInfo) {
    contentCommands.push('0.94 0.96 0.99 rg 415 570 140 140 re f');
    contentCommands.push('0.82 0.86 0.92 RG 1 w 415 570 140 140 re S');
    contentCommands.push(`q 128 0 0 128 421 576 cm /Im1 Do Q`);
    contentCommands.push('BT /F1 8.5 Tf 0.34 0.43 0.56 rg 438 558 Td (LIVE VERIFIED PHOTO) Tj ET');
  }

  const asciiClean = (text) =>
    String(text || '')
      .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim() || String(employee.employeeCode || 'Staff');

  const escapePdfStr = (str) =>
    asciiClean(str).replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/\(/g, '\\(').replace(/\)/g, '\\)');

  const rows = [
    ['Report ID', logEntry.id],
    ['Employee', `${employee.name} (${employee.employeeCode})`],
    ['Department', employee.department],
    ['Role Title', employee.roleTitle || 'Authorized Staff'],
    ['Attendance Type', 'CHECK-OUT (Verified)'],
    ['Verification', 'Live Biometric Face Scan'],
    ['Confidence', `${logEntry.confidenceScore ?? 99}%`],
    ['Timestamp', `${logEntry.loggedTimeLabel}  |  ${logEntry.loggedDateKey}`],
    ['Cloud Sync', logEntry.syncedToCloud ? 'Synced (attendanceapp-f6a0e)' : 'Recorded Offline'],
    ['Operator', operatorEmail || 'Device Web Browser'],
    ['Recipient', TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL]
  ];

  let y = 682;
  for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const [label, value] = rows[i];
    const rowWidth = jpegInfo && y > 545 ? 360 : 515;
    if (i % 2 === 0) {
      contentCommands.push(`0.961 0.973 0.988 rg 40 ${y - 6} ${rowWidth} 22 re f`);
    }
    contentCommands.push(`0.824 0.863 0.922 RG 0.8 w 40 ${y - 6} ${rowWidth} 22 re S`);
    contentCommands.push(`BT /F2 9.5 Tf 0.337 0.431 0.565 rg 48 ${y + 1} Td (${escapePdfStr(label)}) Tj ET`);
    contentCommands.push(`BT /F1 10 Tf 0.071 0.110 0.176 rg 148 ${y + 1} Td (${escapePdfStr(value)}) Tj ET`);
    y -= 24;
  }

  contentCommands.push(
    'BT /F1 8.5 Tf 0.45 0.53 0.65 rg 40 42 Td (Generated by NICH Employee Attendance 3 GitHub Pages Web Terminal) Tj ET'
  );

  const streamString = contentCommands.join('\n');
  const streamBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(streamString);

  // Assemble binary PDF objects safely
  const chunks = [];
  const offsets = [0];
  let byteLength = 0;

  const pushStr = (s) => {
    const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(s);
    chunks.push(encoded);
    byteLength += encoded.length;
  };
  const pushBytes = (bytes) => {
    chunks.push(bytes);
    byteLength += bytes.length;
  };

  pushStr('%PDF-1.4\n');

  const xobjectRef = jpegInfo ? '/XObject << /Im1 6 0 R >> ' : '';

  // 1: Catalog
  offsets[1] = byteLength;
  pushStr('1 0 obj\n<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>\nendobj\n');

  // 2: Pages
  offsets[2] = byteLength;
  pushStr('2 0 obj\n<< /Type /Pages /Kids [3 0 R] /Count 1 >>\nendobj\n');

  // 3: Page
  offsets[3] = byteLength;
  pushStr(
    `3 0 obj\n<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 ${pageWidth} ${pageHeight}] ` +
      `/Resources << /Font << /F1 4 0 R /F2 5 0 R >> ${xobjectRef}>> /Contents ${jpegInfo ? 7 : 6} 0 R >>\nendobj\n`
  );

  // 4: Helvetica Regular
  offsets[4] = byteLength;
  pushStr('4 0 obj\n<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica >>\nendobj\n');

  // 5: Helvetica Bold
  offsets[5] = byteLength;
  pushStr('5 0 obj\n<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica-Bold >>\nendobj\n');

  // 6 (optional): JPEG Image XObject
  const contentObjId = jpegInfo ? 7 : 6;
  if (jpegInfo) {
    offsets[6] = byteLength;
    pushStr(
      `6 0 obj\n<< /Type /XObject /Subtype /Image /Width ${jpegInfo.width} /Height ${jpegInfo.height} ` +
        `/ColorSpace /DeviceRGB /BitsPerComponent 8 /Filter /DCTDecode /Length ${jpegInfo.bytes.length} >>\nstream\n`
    );
    pushBytes(jpegInfo.bytes);
    pushStr('\nendstream\nendobj\n');
  }

  // Content stream
  offsets[contentObjId] = byteLength;
  pushStr(`${contentObjId} 0 obj\n<< /Length ${streamBytes.length} >>\nstream\n`);
  pushBytes(streamBytes);
  pushStr('\nendstream\nendobj\n');

  // Cross-reference table
  const xrefStart = byteLength;
  const totalObjs = contentObjId + 1;
  pushStr(`xref\n0 ${totalObjs}\n0000000000 65535 f \n`);
  for (let id = 1; id < totalObjs; id++) {
    pushStr(`${String(offsets[id]).padStart(10, '0')} 00000 n \n`);
  }
  pushStr(`trailer\n<< /Size ${totalObjs} /Root 1 0 R >>\nstartxref\n${xrefStart}\n%%EOF`);

  return new Blob(chunks, { type: 'application/pdf' });
}

async function extractJpegInfoFromDataUrl(dataUrl) {
  if (!dataUrl || typeof dataUrl !== 'string' || !dataUrl.startsWith('data:image/jpeg')) {
    return null;
  }
  try {
    const base64 = dataUrl.split(',')[1];
    if (!base64) return null;
    const bin = atob(base64);
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) {
      bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
    }

    // Parse JPEG SOF0/SOF2 dimensions
    let i = 2;
    while (i + 9 < bytes.length) {
      if (bytes[i] !== 0xff) {
        i++;
        continue;
      }
      const marker = bytes[i + 1];
      if (marker === 0xc0 || marker === 0xc2) {
        const height = (bytes[i + 5] << 8) | bytes[i + 6];
        const width = (bytes[i + 7] << 8) | bytes[i + 8];
        if (width > 0 && height > 0) {
          return { bytes, width, height };
        }
        return null;
      }
      if (marker === 0xd8 || marker === 0xd9 || marker === 0x01 || (marker >= 0xd0 && marker <= 0xd7)) {
        i += 2;
      } else {
        const segLen = (bytes[i + 2] << 8) | bytes[i + 3];
        if (segLen < 2) break;
        i += 2 + segLen;
      }
    }
    return null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}


======================================================================
// FILE: docs/js/scanner-overlay.js
======================================================================
/**
 * Canvas renderer mirroring Android BiometricCameraScanner.kt:
 * - Precision double-stroked blue/cyan boundary L-brackets
 * - Concentric 3D geometric facial wireframe mesh with pulsing nodes
 * - Oscillating volumetric blue horizontal scan beam
 */

const SCANNER_ELECTRIC_BLUE = '#29B6F6';
const SCANNER_DEEP_BLUE = '#1E88E5';
const SCANNER_NODE_CYAN = '#80D8FF';
const NEON_CYAN = '#00E5FF';
const NODE_MAGENTA = '#FF80EC';

export function startScannerOverlayLoop(canvas) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  let rafId = 0;
  let running = true;
  let faceDetectedHint = false;
  const startTime = performance.now();

  function resizeCanvas() {
    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    const width = canvas.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
    const height = canvas.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
    if (canvas.width !== Math.round(width * dpr) || canvas.height !== Math.round(height * dpr)) {
      canvas.width = Math.round(width * dpr);
      canvas.height = Math.round(height * dpr);
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    }
    return { width, height };
  }

  function drawFrame(now) {
    if (!running) return;
    const { width: w, height: h } = resizeCanvas();
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

    // Exact proportions matching BiometricCameraScanner.kt
    const boxW = Math.min(w * 0.72, h * 0.44);
    const boxH = Math.min(boxW * 1.24, h * 0.62);
    const left = (w - boxW) * 0.5;
    const top = (h - boxH) * 0.46;
    const right = left + boxW;
    const bottom = top + boxH;
    const cornerLen = boxW * 0.22;

    const sweepFraction = 0.5 - 0.5 * Math.cos(((now - startTime) % 1900) / 1900 * Math.PI * 2);
    const meshPulse = 0.42 + 0.46 * (0.5 - 0.5 * Math.cos(((now - startTime) % 1400) / 1400 * Math.PI * 2));

    drawCornerBrackets(ctx, left, top, right, bottom, cornerLen, faceDetectedHint);
    drawGeometricFacialMesh(ctx, left, top, boxW, boxH, meshPulse, faceDetectedHint);
    drawVolumetricSweepBeam(ctx, left, top, boxW, boxH, sweepFraction);

    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(drawFrame);
  }

  rafId = requestAnimationFrame(drawFrame);

  return {
    setFaceDetected(detected) {
      faceDetectedHint = Boolean(detected);
    },
    stop() {
      running = false;
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
    }
  };
}

function drawCornerBrackets(ctx, left, top, right, bottom, len, locked) {
  const stroke = (color, width) => {
    ctx.strokeStyle = color;
    ctx.lineWidth = width;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.beginPath();

    // Top-left
    ctx.moveTo(left, top + len);
    ctx.lineTo(left, top);
    ctx.lineTo(left + len, top);

    // Top-right
    ctx.moveTo(right - len, top);
    ctx.lineTo(right, top);
    ctx.lineTo(right, top + len);

    // Bottom-left
    ctx.moveTo(left, bottom - len);
    ctx.lineTo(left, bottom);
    ctx.lineTo(left + len, bottom);

    // Bottom-right
    ctx.moveTo(right - len, bottom);
    ctx.lineTo(right, bottom);
    ctx.lineTo(right, bottom - len);

    ctx.stroke();
  };

  stroke(locked ? 'rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.34)' : 'rgba(41, 182, 246, 0.30)', 9);
  stroke(locked ? NEON_CYAN : SCANNER_ELECTRIC_BLUE, 3.2);
}

function drawGeometricFacialMesh(ctx, left, top, boxW, boxH, pulse, locked) {
  const cx = left + boxW * 0.5;
  const cy = top + boxH * 0.48;
  const rx = boxW * 0.31;
  const ry = boxH * 0.36;

  const primaryAlpha = (0.36 + 0.34 * pulse).toFixed(2);
  const secondaryAlpha = (0.20 + 0.22 * pulse).toFixed(2);

  ctx.save();
  ctx.strokeStyle = locked
    ? `rgba(0, 229, 255, ${primaryAlpha})`
    : `rgba(41, 182, 246, ${primaryAlpha})`;
  ctx.lineWidth = 1.4;

  // Concentric oval facial contour bands
  for (const scale of [1.0, 0.72, 0.44]) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(cx, cy, rx * scale, ry * scale, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // Meridian longitude lines across face
  ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(30, 136, 229, ${secondaryAlpha})`;
  ctx.lineWidth = 1.1;
  for (const curveFactor of [-0.62, -0.32, 0, 0.32, 0.62]) {
    const xOffset = rx * curveFactor;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx + xOffset * 0.48, cy - ry);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx + xOffset * 1.15, cy, cx + xOffset * 0.44, cy + ry);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // Latitude contour ribs (forehead, eyes, nose bridge, mouth, chin)
  for (const yFactor of [-0.65, -0.32, 0, 0.34, 0.68]) {
    const yLine = cy + ry * yFactor;
    const halfSpan = rx * Math.sqrt(Math.max(0.05, 1 - yFactor * yFactor * 0.85));
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx - halfSpan, yLine);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, yLine + ry * 0.04, cx + halfSpan, yLine);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // Facial keypoint anchor nodes matching Android wireframe triangulation
  const nodes = [
    [cx, cy - ry * 0.88],
    [cx - rx * 0.42, cy - ry * 0.44],
    [cx + rx * 0.42, cy - ry * 0.44],
    [cx - rx * 0.38, cy - ry * 0.22],
    [cx + rx * 0.38, cy - ry * 0.22],
    [cx, cy - ry * 0.04],
    [cx - rx * 0.56, cy + ry * 0.14],
    [cx + rx * 0.56, cy + ry * 0.14],
    [cx - rx * 0.28, cy + ry * 0.42],
    [cx + rx * 0.28, cy + ry * 0.42],
    [cx, cy + ry * 0.88]
  ];

  const connections = [
    [0, 1], [0, 2], [1, 3], [2, 4], [3, 5], [4, 5],
    [1, 6], [2, 7], [3, 6], [4, 7], [5, 8], [5, 9],
    [6, 8], [7, 9], [8, 10], [9, 10], [3, 4], [8, 9]
  ];

  ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(128, 216, 255, ${(0.28 + 0.3 * pulse).toFixed(2)})`;
  ctx.lineWidth = 1.25;
  ctx.beginPath();
  for (const [a, b] of connections) {
    ctx.moveTo(nodes[a][0], nodes[a][1]);
    ctx.lineTo(nodes[b][0], nodes[b][1]);
  }
  ctx.stroke();

  // Glowing node markers (highlight inner eyes/nose/mouth points matching EZFace reference)
  nodes.forEach(([nx, ny], idx) => {
    const isHighlight = idx === 2 || idx === 4 || idx === 5 || idx === 8 || idx === 9;
    ctx.fillStyle = isHighlight
      ? `rgba(255, 128, 236, ${(0.62 + 0.32 * pulse).toFixed(2)})`
      : `rgba(128, 216, 255, ${(0.55 + 0.35 * pulse).toFixed(2)})`;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(nx, ny, isHighlight ? 3.4 : 2.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  });

  ctx.restore();
}

function drawVolumetricSweepBeam(ctx, left, top, boxW, boxH, sweepFraction) {
  const beamY = top + 10 + (boxH - 20) * sweepFraction;
  const beamHalfBand = 24;

  const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, beamY - beamHalfBand, 0, beamY + beamHalfBand);
  grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(41, 182, 246, 0)');
  grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(41, 182, 246, 0.34)');
  grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(41, 182, 246, 0)');

  ctx.fillStyle = grad;
  ctx.fillRect(left + 6, beamY - beamHalfBand, boxW - 12, beamHalfBand * 2);

  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(128, 216, 255, 0.92)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2.4;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(left + 6, beamY);
  ctx.lineTo(left + boxW - 6, beamY);
  ctx.stroke();
}
